/**
 * Atualiza o horÃ¡rio, telefone e subtÃ­tulo/aviso do restaurante e revalida o menu pÃºblico.
 */
export async function updateRestaurantSettings(
  input: z.infer<typeof updateRestaurantSettingsSchema>
): Promise<ProductStockActionResult<RestaurantSettingsData>> {
  try {
    const parsed = updateRestaurantSettingsSchema.safeParse(input);
    if (!parsed.success) {
      return {
        success: false,
        error: {
          code: 'VALIDATION_ERROR',
          message: parsed.error.issues[0]?.message || 'Dados invÃ¡lidos.',
        },
      };
    }

    const staff = await getAuthenticatedStaff();
    if (!staff || !isOwner(staff)) {
      return {
        success: false,
        error: { code: 'UNAUTHORIZED', message: 'SessÃ£o expirada ou utilizador nÃ£o autorizado.' },
      };
    }

    const supabase = await createServerSupabaseClient();
    if (!supabase) {
      return {
        success: false,
        error: { code: 'DATABASE_ERROR', message: 'ServiÃ§o de base de dados indisponÃ­vel.' },
      };
    }

    const { data: updated, error } = await supabase
      .from('restaurants')
      .update({
        opening_hours: parsed.data.openingHours,
        phone: parsed.data.phone?.trim() || null,
        tagline: parsed.data.tagline?.trim() || null,
        updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      })
      .eq('id', staff.restaurant.id)
      .select('opening_hours, phone, tagline')
      .maybeSingle();

    if (error || !updated) {
      return {
        success: false,
        error: { code: 'UPDATE_FAILED', message: 'NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel guardar as definiÃ§Ãµes.' },
      };
    }

    revalidateMenuAndAdmin(staff.restaurant.slug);

    return {
      success: true,
      data: {
        openingHours: updated.opening_hours || '',
        phone: updated.phone || '',
        tagline: updated.tagline || '',
      },
    };
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    console.error('[updateRestaurantSettings] ExceÃ§Ã£o:', err);
    return {
      success: false,
      error: { code: 'UNEXPECTED_ERROR', message: 'Erro inesperado ao guardar definiÃ§Ãµes.' },
    };
  }
}


========== src/lib/auth/staff.ts ==========

import { cache } from 'react';
import { createServerSupabaseClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';

export type StaffRole = 'owner' | 'manager' | 'staff';

export interface AuthenticatedStaff {
  userId: string;
  email: string;
  profile: {
    id: string;
    fullName: string | null;
    role: StaffRole;
    restaurantId: string;
  };
  restaurant: {
    id: string;
    name: string;
    fullName: string;
    slug: string;
    isActive: boolean;
    isOpen: boolean;
  };
}

const ALLOWED_ROLES: StaffRole[] = ['owner', 'manager', 'staff'];

/**
 * Validates the current user session server-side (deduplicated per request via React.cache).
 * Verifies that the user exists in auth.users, has an active profile in public.profiles,
 * has a valid role ('owner' | 'manager' | 'staff'), and belongs to an active restaurant.
 *
 * Returns safe staff information or null if unauthenticated or unauthorized.
 */
export const getAuthenticatedStaff = cache(
  async (): Promise<AuthenticatedStaff | null> => {
    const supabase = await createServerSupabaseClient();
    if (!supabase) {
      return null;
    }

    // 1. Get authenticated user from session cookie
    const {
      data: { user },
      error: userError,
    } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

    if (userError || !user) {
      return null;
    }

    // 2. Fetch profile and associated restaurant in a single relational query
    const { data: profile, error: profileError } = await supabase
      .from('profiles')
      .select(
        `
        id,
        full_name,
        role,
        restaurant_id,
        restaurants (
          id,
          name,
          full_name,
          slug,
          is_active,
          is_open
        )
      `
      )
      .eq('id', user.id)
      .single();

    if (profileError || !profile || !profile.restaurant_id) {
      console.warn(
        `[getAuthenticatedStaff]: Perfil nÃ£o encontrado ou sem restaurante para user ${user.id}:`,
        profileError?.message
      );
      return null;
    }

    // 3. Verify role authorization
    if (!ALLOWED_ROLES.includes(profile.role as StaffRole)) {
      console.warn(
        `[getAuthenticatedStaff]: Cargo nÃ£o autorizado ("${profile.role}") para user ${user.id}. Permitidos:`,
        ALLOWED_ROLES
      );
      return null;
    }

    const joinedRestaurant = Array.isArray(profile.restaurants)
      ? profile.restaurants[0]
      : profile.restaurants;

    if (!joinedRestaurant) {
      console.warn(
        `[getAuthenticatedStaff]: Restaurante ${profile.restaurant_id} nÃ£o encontrado ou inativo.`
      );
      return null;
    }

    return {
      userId: user.id,
      email: user.email ?? '',
      profile: {
        id: profile.id,
        fullName: profile.full_name,
        role: profile.role as StaffRole,
        restaurantId: profile.restaurant_id,
      },
      restaurant: {
        id: joinedRestaurant.id,
        name: joinedRestaurant.name,
        fullName: joinedRestaurant.full_name,
        slug: joinedRestaurant.slug,
        isActive: joinedRestaurant.is_active,
        isOpen: joinedRestaurant.is_open,
      },
    };
  }
);

/**
 * Helper RBAC para verificar se o utilizador autenticado Ã© o Dono do restaurante.
 * Apenas utilizadores com role === 'owner' tÃªm privilÃ©gios administrativos totais.
 */
export function isOwner(
  staff: AuthenticatedStaff | null
): staff is AuthenticatedStaff {
  if (!staff) return false;
  return staff.profile.role === 'owner';
}

/**
 * Helper RBAC para controlo de acesso administrativo e analÃ­tico.
 * Apenas o Dono ('owner') tem permissÃ£o para aceder a BI, CatÃ¡logo e GestÃ£o.
 */
export function canManageCatalogAndAnalytics(
  staff: AuthenticatedStaff | null
): staff is AuthenticatedStaff {
  return isOwner(staff);
}


========== src/types/restaurant.ts ==========

export type OrderStatus =
  | 'pending'
  | 'accepted'
  | 'preparing'
  | 'ready'
  | 'completed'
  | 'cancelled';

export type ProductBadge =
  | 'picante'
  | 'vegetariano'
  | 'vegan'
  | 'especialidade'
  | 'novidade'
  | 'house_special'
  | 'popular'
  | 'promocao'
  | 'extra'
  | 'acompanhamento';

export interface Restaurant {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  fullName: string;
  slug: string;
  tagline: string;
  description: string;
  location: string;
  logoUrl?: string;
  coverImageUrl: string;
  currency: string;
  timezone: string;
  address: string;
  phone?: string;
  isOpen: boolean;
  openingHours: string;
  rating?: number;
  reviewCount?: number;
}

export interface Category {
  id: string;
  restaurantId: string;
  name: string;
  slug: string;
  description?: string;
  subtitle?: string;
  position: number;
  active: boolean;
  isSpecialty?: boolean;
}

export interface Product {
  id: string;
  restaurantId: string;
  categoryId: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  price: number; // in Euros
  cost?: number; // estimated ingredient cost for analytics
  imageUrl: string;
  available: boolean;
  position: number;
  badges?: ProductBadge[];
  unitQuantity?: string; // e.g. "6 unidades", "MÃ©dia â€” 31 cm"
  includesNotes?: string; // e.g. "Inclui batata palito"
  customizationNote?: string; // e.g. "OpÃ§Ã£o queijo mozzarella sem custo adicional"
  allergens?: string[];
  prepTimeMinutes?: number;
}

export interface Table {
  id: string;
  restaurantId: string;
  number: number;
  name: string;
  active: boolean;
}

export type ProductModifierGroup = 'extra' | 'remove' | 'sauce';

export interface ProductModifier {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  priceDelta: number;
  group: ProductModifierGroup;
}

export interface CartItem {
  id?: string;
  product: Product;
  quantity: number;
  notes?: string;
  selectedModifiers?: ProductModifier[];
}

export function getCartItemUnitPrice(item: CartItem): number {
  const modifiersDelta = (item.selectedModifiers || []).reduce(
    (sum, mod) => sum + (mod.priceDelta || 0),
    0
  );
  return Number((item.product.price + modifiersDelta).toFixed(2));
}

export function getCartItemTotal(item: CartItem): number {
  return Number((getCartItemUnitPrice(item) * item.quantity).toFixed(2));
}

export interface Order {
  id: string;
  orderNumber?: number;
  restaurantId?: string;
  restaurantSlug?: string;
  restaurantName?: string;
  tableId?: string;
  tableNumber: number;
  tableName?: string;
  items: CartItem[];
  subtotal: number;
  total: number;
  status: OrderStatus;
  createdAt: string;
  customerNotes?: string;
  trackingToken?: string;
}

'use client';

import React, {
  useCallback,

  useMemo,

import {
  ManagedOrder,

interface OrderDashboardProps {
  restaurantId: string;

  restaurantSlug: string;
  initialIsRestaurantOpen?: boolean;
  initialOrders: ManagedOrder[];
  isOwner: boolean;
}

type SectionFilter = 'all' | 'bar' | 'kitchen';
type KdsViewMode = 'orders' | 'tables';

interface TableServiceCall {
  id: string;
  tableNumber: number;
  type: 'bill_card' | 'bill_cash' | 'call_staff';
  label: string;
  nif?: string | null;
  createdAt: string;
}

const SECTION_FILTERS: Array<{
  key: SectionFilter;
  label: string;
}> = [
    {
      key: 'all',
      label: 'Todos',
    },
    {
      key: 'bar',
      label: 'Bar / Copa',
    },
    {
      key: 'kitchen',
      label: 'Cozinha',
    },
  ];

export function OrderDashboard({
  restaurantId,
  restaurantName,
  restaurantSlug,
  initialIsRestaurantOpen = true,
  initialOrders,
  isOwner,
}: OrderDashboardProps) {
  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<ManagedOrder[]>(initialOrders);
  const [isRestaurantOpen, setIsRestaurantOpen] = useState(
    initialIsRestaurantOpen
  );
  const [isTogglingOpen, setIsTogglingOpen] = useState(false);
  const [printingOrder, setPrintingOrder] = useState<ManagedOrder | null>(null);
  const [autoPrintOnAccept, setAutoPrintOnAccept] = useState(false);
  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<KdsViewMode>('orders');
  const [tableCalls, setTableCalls] = useState<TableServiceCall[]>([]);
  const tableCallsStorageKey = `gulas_kds_table_calls_${restaurantSlug || 'gulas'}`;
  const isTableCallsHydratedRef = useRef(false);
  const lastLocalMutationAtRef = useRef<number>(0);
  const statusBroadcastChannelRef = useRef<RealtimeChannel | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [dashboardError, setDashboardError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [, setLastRefreshedAt] = useState<Date | null>(null);
  const [updatingMap, setUpdatingMap] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});
  const [orderErrorMap, setOrderErrorMap] = useState<
    Record<string, string | null>
  >({});
  const [newOrderIds, setNewOrderIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [sectionFilter, setSectionFilter] = useState<SectionFilter>('all');
  const [kdsSearchQuery, setKdsSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [onlyUrgentSla, setOnlyUrgentSla] = useState(false);
  const [nowMs, setNowMs] = useState<number>(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    const syncClock = () => setNowMs(Date.now());
    const initTimer = setTimeout(syncClock, 0);
    const interval = setInterval(syncClock, 15000);
    return () => {
      clearTimeout(initTimer);
      clearInterval(interval);
    };
  }, []);
  const {
    isSupported: isWakeLockSupported,
    isActive: isWakeLockActive,
    status: wakeLockStatus,
    requestWakeLock,
    releaseWakeLock,
  } = useScreenWakeLock(true);
  const [a11yAnnouncement, setA11yAnnouncement] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      const savedAutoPrint = localStorage.getItem('gulas_kds_autoprint');
      if (savedAutoPrint === 'true') {
        queueMicrotask(() => setAutoPrintOnAccept(true));
      }
    } catch {
      // Ignorar
    }
  }, []);

  const toggleAutoPrint = () => {
    setAutoPrintOnAccept((prev) => {
      const next = !prev;
      try {
        localStorage.setItem('gulas_kds_autoprint', String(next));
      } catch {
        // Ignorar
      }
      return next;
    });
  };

  const previousOrderIdsRef = useRef<Set<string>>(
    new Set(initialOrders.map((order) => order.id))
  );
  const isInitialLoadRef = useRef(true);
  const highlightTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
  const announcementTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(
    null
  );

  const [isQrModalOpen, setIsQrModalOpen] = useState(false);

  const [, startRefreshTransition] = useTransition();
  const {
    alertsState,
    activateAlerts,
    deactivateAlerts,
    triggerNewOrderAlert,
  } = useKdsAlerts();

  // SubscriÃ§Ã£o em tempo real para Chamadas de Mesa ("Pedir a Conta" / "Chamar Staff") + Canal de Broadcast de Estado KDS -> Cliente
  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      const rawCalls = localStorage.getItem(tableCallsStorageKey);
      if (rawCalls) {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(rawCalls);
        if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {
          const twoHoursMs = 2 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
          const nowMs = Date.now();
          const validCalls = parsed.filter((c): c is TableServiceCall => {
            if (!c || typeof c.id !== 'string' || typeof c.tableNumber !== 'number') {
              return false;
            }
            const age = nowMs - new Date(c.createdAt).getTime();
            return !Number.isNaN(age) && age >= 0 && age < twoHoursMs;
          });
          if (validCalls.length > 0) {
            queueMicrotask(() => setTableCalls(validCalls));
          }
        }
      }
    } catch {
      // Ignorar
    } finally {
      isTableCallsHydratedRef.current = true;
    }

    const supabase = createClient();
    if (!supabase) return;

    const tableChannel = supabase
      .channel(`table-service-${restaurantSlug || 'gulas'}`)
      .on(
        'broadcast',
        { event: 'table_call' },
        (msg) => {
          const call = msg?.payload as TableServiceCall | undefined;
          if (!call || !call.tableNumber) return;

          setTableCalls((prev) => [
            call,
            ...prev.filter((c) => c.id !== call.id),
          ]);
          triggerNewOrderAlert();
        }
      )
      .subscribe();

    const statusChannel = supabase
      .channel('gulas-order-status-broadcast')
      .subscribe();
    statusBroadcastChannelRef.current = statusChannel;

    return () => {
      statusBroadcastChannelRef.current = null;
      void supabase.removeChannel(tableChannel);
      void supabase.removeChannel(statusChannel);
    };
  }, [restaurantSlug, tableCallsStorageKey, triggerNewOrderAlert]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isTableCallsHydratedRef.current) return;
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(tableCallsStorageKey, JSON.stringify(tableCalls));
    } catch {
      // Ignorar
    }
  }, [tableCalls, tableCallsStorageKey]);

  interface UndoOrderState {
    orderId: string;
    orderNumber: number;
    tableNumber: number;
    updatedAt: string;
  }

  const [undoOrder, setUndoOrder] = useState<UndoOrderState | null>(null);
  const [isUndoing, setIsUndoing] = useState(false);
  const undoTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const mountTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      setLastRefreshedAt(new Date());
    }, 0);

    return () => {
      clearTimeout(mountTimer);
      if (highlightTimerRef.current) {
        clearTimeout(highlightTimerRef.current);
      }
      if (announcementTimerRef.current) {
        clearTimeout(announcementTimerRef.current);
      }
      if (undoTimerRef.current) {
        clearTimeout(undoTimerRef.current);
      }
    };
  }, []);

  const fetchOrders = useCallback(async () => {
    setDashboardError(null);
    setIsLoading(true);

    try {
      const result = await getActiveOrders();

      if (!result.success) {
        setDashboardError(result.error.message);
        return;
      }

      const currentIds = previousOrderIdsRef.current;
      const newlyAdded = new Set<string>();

      result.data.forEach((order) => {
        if (!currentIds.has(order.id)) {
          newlyAdded.add(order.id);
        }
      });

      if (newlyAdded.size > 0) {
        setNewOrderIds((previous) => new Set([...previous, ...newlyAdded]));

        if (highlightTimerRef.current) {
          clearTimeout(highlightTimerRef.current);
        }

        highlightTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
          setNewOrderIds((previous) => {
            const next = new Set(previous);
            newlyAdded.forEach((id) => {
              next.delete(id);
            });
            return next;
          });
        }, 10000);
      }

      previousOrderIdsRef.current = new Set(
        result.data.map((order) => order.id)
      );
      setOrders(result.data);
      setLastRefreshedAt(new Date());
    } catch {
      setDashboardError('NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel carregar os pedidos.');
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
      isInitialLoadRef.current = false;
    }
  }, []);

  const handleNewOrder = useCallback(
    (orderId: string) => {
      if (isInitialLoadRef.current) {
        return;
      }

      if (previousOrderIdsRef.current.has(orderId)) {
        return;
      }

      // Garante que um novo pedido vindo de fora nunca Ã© ignorado pelo cooldown de mutaÃ§Ã£o local
      lastLocalMutationAtRef.current = 0;

      triggerNewOrderAlert();
      setA11yAnnouncement('Novo pedido recebido.');

      if (announcementTimerRef.current) {
        clearTimeout(announcementTimerRef.current);
      }

      announcementTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
        setA11yAnnouncement('');
      }, 2000);
    },
    [triggerNewOrderAlert]
  );

  const markLocalMutation = useCallback(() => {
    lastLocalMutationAtRef.current = Date.now();
  }, []);

  const handleRealtimeOrdersChange = useCallback(async () => {
    // Evita refetch completo duplicado imediatamente apÃ³s uma mutaÃ§Ã£o feita neste tablet
    if (Date.now() - lastLocalMutationAtRef.current < 1500) {
      return;
    }
    await fetchOrders();
  }, [fetchOrders]);

  const { isConnected } = useOrdersRealtime({
    restaurantId,
    onOrdersChange: handleRealtimeOrdersChange,
    onNewOrder: handleNewOrder,
  });

  // Fallback Polling AutomÃ¡tico se o WebSocket cair
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isConnected) return;

    const fallbackInterval = setInterval(() => {
      if (typeof document !== 'undefined' && document.visibilityState === 'hidden') {
        return;
      }
      void fetchOrders();
    }, 10000);

    return () => clearInterval(fallbackInterval);
  }, [isConnected, fetchOrders]);

  const handleToggleRestaurantOpen = async () => {
    if (isTogglingOpen) return;
    setIsTogglingOpen(true);
    const nextOpen = !isRestaurantOpen;
    try {
      const res = await toggleRestaurantOpenStatus(nextOpen);
      if (res.success) {
        setIsRestaurantOpen(res.data.isOpen);
      } else {
        setDashboardError(res.error.message);
      }
    } catch {
      setDashboardError('NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel alterar o estado do restaurante.');
    } finally {
      setIsTogglingOpen(false);
    }
  };

  const handlePrintOrder = useCallback((orderToPrint: ManagedOrder) => {
    setPrintingOrder(orderToPrint);
    setTimeout(() => {
      window.print();
    }, 80);
  }, []);

  const handleManualRefresh = useCallback(() => {
    startRefreshTransition(async () => {
      await fetchOrders();
    });
  }, [fetchOrders]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onGlobalRefresh = () => {
      handleManualRefresh();
    };
    window.addEventListener('gulas:admin-refresh', onGlobalRefresh);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('gulas:admin-refresh', onGlobalRefresh);
    };
  }, [handleManualRefresh]);

  const handleUpdateStatus = async (
    orderId: string,
    nextStatus: ValidNextStatus
  ) => {
    setUpdatingMap((previous) => ({ ...previous, [orderId]: true }));
    setOrderErrorMap((previous) => ({ ...previous, [orderId]: null }));

    try {
      const currentOrder = orders.find((order) => order.id === orderId);

      if (!currentOrder) {
        setOrderErrorMap((previous) => ({
          ...previous,
          [orderId]: 'Este pedido jÃ¡ nÃ£o estÃ¡ disponÃ­vel. Atualize a lista.',
        }));
        return;
      }

      markLocalMutation();

      const result = await updateOrderStatus({
        orderId,
        nextStatus,
        expectedUpdatedAt: currentOrder.updatedAt,
      });

      if (!result.success) {
        if (result.error.code === 'OPTIMISTIC_CONFLICT') {
          await fetchOrders();
        }

        setOrderErrorMap((previous) => ({
          ...previous,
          [orderId]: result.error.message,
        }));
        return;
      }

      // Emite broadcast instantÃ¢neo (<100ms) para o telemÃ³vel do cliente sem esperar pelo fallback polling
      void statusBroadcastChannelRef.current?.send({
        type: 'broadcast',
        event: 'status_update',
        payload: {
          id: result.data.id,
          status: result.data.status,
        },
      });

      setNewOrderIds((previous) => {
        const next = new Set(previous);
        next.delete(orderId);
        return next;
      });

      if (nextStatus === 'accepted' && autoPrintOnAccept) {
        handlePrintOrder(result.data);
      }

      if (nextStatus === 'completed' || nextStatus === 'cancelled') {
        setOrders((previous) =>
          previous.filter((order) => order.id !== orderId)
        );
        previousOrderIdsRef.current.delete(orderId);

        if (nextStatus === 'completed' && isOwner) {
          if (undoTimerRef.current) {
            clearTimeout(undoTimerRef.current);
          }
          setUndoOrder({
            orderId: currentOrder.id,
            orderNumber: currentOrder.orderNumber,
            tableNumber: currentOrder.tableNumber,
            updatedAt: result.data.updatedAt,
          });
          undoTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
            setUndoOrder(null);
          }, 6000);
        }
      } else {
        setOrders((previous) =>
          previous.map((order) =>
            order.id === orderId ? result.data : order
          )
        );
        previousOrderIdsRef.current.add(result.data.id);
      }

      setLastRefreshedAt(new Date());
    } catch {
      setOrderErrorMap((previous) => ({
        ...previous,
        [orderId]: 'Ocorreu um erro ao atualizar o pedido.',
      }));
    } finally {
      setUpdatingMap((previous) => ({ ...previous, [orderId]: false }));
    }
  };

  const handleCloseAllTableOrders = async (tableNum: number) => {
    if (!isOwner) return;

    const tableOrders = orders.filter(
      (order) => order.tableNumber === tableNum
    );

    for (const order of tableOrders) {
      await handleUpdateStatus(order.id, 'completed');
    }
  };


  const handleChangeOrderTable = async (
    orderId: string,
    newTableNumber: number
  ) => {
    setUpdatingMap((previous) => ({ ...previous, [orderId]: true }));
    setOrderErrorMap((previous) => ({ ...previous, [orderId]: null }));
    markLocalMutation();

    try {
      const res = await changeOrderTable({ orderId, newTableNumber });
      if (!res.success) {
        setOrderErrorMap((previous) => ({
          ...previous,
          [orderId]: res.error.message,
        }));
        return;
      }

      setOrders((previous) =>
        previous.map((order) => (order.id === orderId ? res.data : order))
      );
      setA11yAnnouncement(
        `Pedido #${res.data.orderNumber} transferido para ${newTableNumber === 0 ? 'Take-Away' : `Mesa ${newTableNumber}`
        }.`
      );
    } catch {
      setOrderErrorMap((previous) => ({
        ...previous,
        [orderId]: 'Erro ao transferir o pedido de mesa.',
      }));
    } finally {
      setUpdatingMap((previous) => ({ ...previous, [orderId]: false }));
    }
  };

  const handleUndo = async () => {
    if (!undoOrder || isUndoing) return;

    setIsUndoing(true);
    markLocalMutation();
    try {
      const result = await reopenCompletedOrder({
        orderId: undoOrder.orderId,
        expectedUpdatedAt: undoOrder.updatedAt,
      });

      if (!result.success) {
        if (result.error.code === 'OPTIMISTIC_CONFLICT') {
          await fetchOrders();
        }
        setDashboardError(result.error.message);
        return;
      }

      void statusBroadcastChannelRef.current?.send({
        type: 'broadcast',
        event: 'status_update',
        payload: {
          id: result.data.id,
          status: result.data.status,
        },
      });

      setOrders((previous) => [
        result.data,
        ...previous.filter((order) => order.id !== result.data.id),
      ]);
      previousOrderIdsRef.current.add(result.data.id);
      setA11yAnnouncement(
        `Pedido #${result.data.orderNumber} reaberto com sucesso.`
      );

      if (undoTimerRef.current) {
        clearTimeout(undoTimerRef.current);
      }
      setUndoOrder(null);
    } catch {
      setDashboardError('NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel reverter a conclusÃ£o do pedido.');
    } finally {
      setIsUndoing(false);
    }
  };

  const pendingCount = orders.filter((order) => order.status === 'pending').length;
  const preparingCount = orders.filter(
    (order) => order.status === 'accepted' || order.status === 'preparing'
  ).length;
  const readyCount = orders.filter((order) => order.status === 'ready').length;
  const totalActiveCount = orders.length;

  const filteredOrders = orders.filter((order) => {
    if (sectionFilter !== 'all') {
      const hasBarItem = order.items.some(
        (item) => item.categorySlug === 'bebidas'
      );
      const hasKitchenItem = order.items.some(
        (item) => item.categorySlug !== 'bebidas'
      );

      if (sectionFilter === 'bar' && !hasBarItem) return false;
      if (sectionFilter === 'kitchen' && !hasKitchenItem) return false;
    }

    if (onlyUrgentSla && nowMs > 0) {
      const elapsedMs = nowMs - new Date(order.createdAt).getTime();
      if (elapsedMs < 20 * 60 * 1000) return false;
    }

    const q = kdsSearchQuery.replace('#', '').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (q) {
      const matchesNum = String(order.orderNumber).includes(q);
      const matchesTable =
        order.tableNumber === 0
          ? 'take-away takeaway'.includes(q)
          : `mesa ${order.tableNumber}`.includes(q) || String(order.tableNumber) === q;
      const matchesNotes = Boolean(order.customerNotes?.toLowerCase().includes(q));
      const matchesItem = order.items.some((item) =>
        item.productName.toLowerCase().includes(q)
      );
      if (!matchesNum && !matchesTable && !matchesNotes && !matchesItem) {
        return false;
      }
    }

    return true;
  });

  // Dados estruturados para a Vista de Mapa de Mesas (0 = Take-Away, 1..20 = Mesas)
  const tablesOverview = useMemo(() => {
    const slots = [0, ...Array.from({ length: 20 }, (_, i) => i + 1)];
    return slots.map((tableNumber) => {
      const activeForTable = orders.filter((o) => o.tableNumber === tableNumber);
      const totalBill = activeForTable.reduce((sum, o) => sum + o.total, 0);
      const hasReady = activeForTable.some((o) => o.status === 'ready');
      const hasPending = activeForTable.some((o) => o.status === 'pending');
      const hasCall = tableCalls.some((c) => c.tableNumber === tableNumber);

      return {
        tableNumber,
        label: tableNumber === 0 ? 'Take-Away / BalcÃ£o' : `Mesa ${tableNumber}`,
        orders: activeForTable,
        totalBill,
        hasReady,
        hasPending,
        hasCall,
      };
    });
  }, [orders, tableCalls]);

  return (
    <div
      className="space-y-6 pb-12 min-h-screen bg-transparent text-zinc-900 rounded-none"
    >
      <div
        role="status"
        aria-live="polite"
        aria-atomic="true"
        className="sr-only"
      >
        {a11yAnnouncement}
      </div>

      {/* Alertas ao Vivo de AssistÃªncia na Mesa ("Pedir a Conta" / "Chamar Staff") */}
      {tableCalls.length > 0 && (
        <div className="space-y-2.5">
          {tableCalls.map((call) => (
            <div
              key={call.id}
              className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 p-4 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900 text-white shadow-md animate-in slide-in-from-top-2 duration-200"
            >
              <div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 flex-wrap">
                  <span className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-amber-400 text-zinc-950 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wide">
                    Mesa {call.tableNumber}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-sm font-bold text-white">
                    {call.label}
                  </span>
                  {call.nif && (
                    <span className="text-xs font-mono px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-lg border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-800 text-amber-300">
                      NIF: {call.nif}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
                <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400 mt-1.5">
                  Solicitado pelo cliente na mesa Ã s{' '}
                  {new Date(call.createdAt).toLocaleTimeString('pt-PT', {
                    hour: '2-digit',
                    minute: '2-digit',
                  })}
                </p>
              </div>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() =>
                  setTableCalls((prev) => prev.filter((c) => c.id !== call.id))
                }
                className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl border border-white bg-white hover:bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-950 text-xs font-semibold shadow-2xs transition-all active:scale-[0.98] cursor-pointer shrink-0"
              >
                Marcar Atendido
              </button>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Top Header & Operational Controls */}
      <section
        aria-label="CabeÃ§alho de controlo do Monitor"
        className="rounded-3xl border border-zinc-200/90 bg-white p-5 sm:p-7 shadow-xs"
      >
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight text-zinc-950">
            Monitor de OperaÃ§Ãµes
          </h1>

          {/* OpÃ§Ãµes operacionais em linha Ãºnica, com QR Codes na mesma linha Ã  direita */}
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 w-full">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
              {isOwner && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={isTogglingOpen}
                  onClick={handleToggleRestaurantOpen}
                  className={`inline-flex items-center gap-2 h-10 px-4 cursor-pointer rounded-xl border text-xs font-semibold shadow-2xs transition-all active:scale-[0.98] disabled:opacity-60 ${isRestaurantOpen
                    ? 'bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-200 text-emerald-800 hover:bg-emerald-100'
                    : 'bg-rose-50 border-rose-200 text-rose-800 hover:bg-rose-100'
                    }`}
                >
                  <span
                    className={`h-2 w-2 rounded-full ${isRestaurantOpen ? 'bg-emerald-500' : 'bg-rose-500'
                      }`}
                  />
                  <span>
                    {isRestaurantOpen ? 'ReceÃ§Ã£o: Aberta' : 'ReceÃ§Ã£o: Encerrada'}
                  </span>
                </button>
              )}

              <span
                className={`inline-flex items-center gap-2 h-10 px-4 rounded-xl border text-xs font-semibold shadow-2xs ${isConnected
                  ? 'bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-200 text-emerald-800'
                  : 'bg-zinc-100 border-zinc-200 text-zinc-600'
                  }`}
              >
                <span
                  className={`h-2 w-2 rounded-full ${isConnected ? 'bg-emerald-500 animate-pulse' : 'bg-zinc-400'
                    }`}
                />
                <span>{isConnected ? 'Tempo Real' : 'A Sincronizar...'}</span>
              </span>

              {alertsState === 'idle' ? (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  id="kds-alerts-activate-btn"
                  onClick={activateAlerts}
                  className="h-10 px-4 cursor-pointer rounded-xl border border-zinc-200/90 bg-zinc-50 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 shadow-2xs transition-all hover:bg-zinc-900 hover:border-zinc-900 hover:text-white active:scale-[0.98]"
                >
                  Ativar Som
                </button>
              ) : (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  id="kds-alerts-deactivate-btn"
                  onClick={deactivateAlerts}
                  className="h-10 px-4 cursor-pointer rounded-xl border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 hover:bg-emerald-100 text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-800 shadow-2xs transition-all active:scale-[0.98]"
                >
                  Som Ativo
                </button>
              )}

              {isWakeLockSupported &&
                (isWakeLockActive ? (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => void releaseWakeLock()}
                    className="h-10 px-4 cursor-pointer rounded-xl border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 hover:bg-emerald-100 text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-800 shadow-2xs transition-all active:scale-[0.98]"
                    title="EcrÃ£ mantido ligado"
                  >
                    EcrÃ£ Ativo
                  </button>
                ) : (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => void requestWakeLock()}
                    className="h-10 px-4 cursor-pointer rounded-xl border border-zinc-200/90 bg-zinc-50 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 shadow-2xs transition-all hover:bg-zinc-900 hover:border-zinc-900 hover:text-white active:scale-[0.98]"
                  >
                    {wakeLockStatus === 'error' ? 'Tentar EcrÃ£' : 'Fixar EcrÃ£'}
                  </button>
                ))}

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={toggleAutoPrint}
                className={`h-10 px-4 cursor-pointer rounded-xl border text-xs font-semibold shadow-2xs transition-all active:scale-[0.98] ${autoPrintOnAccept
                  ? 'border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 hover:bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800'
                  : 'bg-zinc-50 border-zinc-200/90 text-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-900 hover:border-zinc-900 hover:text-white'
                  }`}
                title="Imprimir automaticamente o talÃ£o tÃ©rmico (80mm) sempre que um pedido for aceite"
              >
                Auto-Print: {autoPrintOnAccept ? 'ON' : 'OFF'}
              </button>
            </div>

            {isOwner && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setIsQrModalOpen(true)}
                className="ml-auto h-10 px-4 cursor-pointer rounded-xl border border-zinc-200/90 bg-zinc-50 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 shadow-2xs transition-all hover:bg-zinc-900 hover:border-zinc-900 hover:text-white active:scale-[0.98]"
              >
                QR Codes
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* KPI Cards */}
      <section
        aria-label="Resumo operacional em tempo real"
        className="flex flex-row items-stretch gap-2 sm:gap-3 w-full"
      >
        {[
          {
            label: 'Total Ativos',
            value: totalActiveCount,
            borderClass: 'border-zinc-800',
            labelClass: 'text-zinc-700',
          },
          {
            label: 'A Aguardar',
            value: pendingCount,
            borderClass: 'border-amber-500',
            labelClass: 'text-amber-800',
          },
          {
            label: 'Em PreparaÃ§Ã£o',
            value: preparingCount,
            borderClass: 'border-orange-600',
            labelClass: 'text-orange-800',
          },
          {
            label: 'Prontos (Pass)',
            value: readyCount,
            borderClass: 'border-emerald-600',
            labelClass: 'text-emerald-800',
          },
        ].map((kpi) => (
          <div
            key={kpi.label}
            className={`flex-1 min-w-0 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 sm:gap-3 rounded-xl border-2 ${kpi.borderClass} bg-white px-3 sm:px-4 py-2.5 shadow-2xs`}
          >
            <span
              className={`text-[10px] sm:text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider truncate ${kpi.labelClass}`}
            >
              {kpi.label}
            </span>
            <span className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold font-mono tracking-tight text-zinc-950 shrink-0">
              {kpi.value}
            </span>
          </div>
        ))}
      </section>

      {/* Barra de Modo de Vista (Pedidos vs Mapa de Mesas) & Filtro de SecÃ§Ã£o */}
      <section
        aria-label="Filtros e vista do monitor"
        className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200/90 bg-white p-2.5 sm:p-3 shadow-2xs flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2"
      >
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setViewMode('orders')}
            className={`h-10 px-4 cursor-pointer rounded-xl border text-xs font-semibold shadow-2xs transition-all active:scale-[0.98] ${viewMode === 'orders'
              ? 'bg-zinc-900 border-zinc-900 text-white'
              : 'bg-zinc-50 border-zinc-200/90 text-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-900 hover:border-zinc-900 hover:text-white'
              }`}
          >
            Pedidos ({totalActiveCount})
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setViewMode('tables')}
            className={`h-10 px-4 cursor-pointer rounded-xl border text-xs font-semibold shadow-2xs transition-all active:scale-[0.98] ${viewMode === 'tables'
              ? 'bg-zinc-900 border-zinc-900 text-white'
              : 'bg-zinc-50 border-zinc-200/90 text-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-900 hover:border-zinc-900 hover:text-white'
              }`}
          >
            Mapa de Sala (1â€“20)
          </button>
        </div>

        {viewMode === 'orders' && (
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            {/* Pesquisa rÃ¡pida no KDS */}
            <div className="flex items-center bg-zinc-50 border border-zinc-200/90 focus-within:bg-white focus-within:border-zinc-900 h-10 rounded-xl shadow-2xs transition-colors overflow-hidden">
              <input
                type="text"
                value={kdsSearchQuery}
                onChange={(e) => setKdsSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Mesa, #pedido ou cliente..."
                className="h-full px-3.5 bg-transparent text-xs font-medium text-zinc-900 placeholder:text-zinc-400 w-44 sm:w-56 focus:outline-none"
              />
              {kdsSearchQuery && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setKdsSearchQuery('')}
                  className="h-full px-3 border-l border-zinc-200 text-[11px] font-semibold text-zinc-500 hover:text-zinc-950 hover:bg-zinc-100 cursor-pointer transition-colors"
                  aria-label="Limpar pesquisa"
                >
                  Limpar
                </button>
              )}
            </div>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setOnlyUrgentSla((v) => !v)}
              className={`h-10 px-4 cursor-pointer rounded-xl border text-xs font-semibold shadow-2xs transition-all active:scale-[0.98] ${onlyUrgentSla
                ? 'bg-rose-600 border-rose-700 text-white'
                : 'bg-rose-50 border-rose-200 text-rose-800 hover:bg-rose-100'
                }`}
              title="Mostrar apenas pedidos acima de 20 minutos de espera (SLA)"
            >
              Urgentes &gt; 20mn
            </button>

            {SECTION_FILTERS.map(({ key, label }) => (
              <button
                key={key}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setSectionFilter(key)}
                className={`h-10 px-4 cursor-pointer rounded-xl border text-xs font-semibold shadow-2xs transition-all active:scale-[0.98] ${sectionFilter === key
                  ? 'bg-zinc-900 border-zinc-900 text-white'
                  : 'bg-zinc-50 border-zinc-200/90 text-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-900 hover:border-zinc-900 hover:text-white'
                  }`}
              >
                {label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </section>

      {/* Vista 1: Mapa de Sala ao Vivo (Bloco 3) */}
      {viewMode === 'tables' ? (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4 xl:grid-cols-5 gap-4">
          {tablesOverview.map((slot) => {
            const isOccupied = slot.orders.length > 0;
            return (
              <div
                key={slot.tableNumber}
                className={`rounded-2xl border p-4 flex flex-col justify-between gap-3 transition-all ${isOccupied
                  ? slot.hasReady
                    ? 'bg-gradient-to-b from-emerald-50/70 to-white border-emerald-300 shadow-xs'
                    : 'bg-white border-zinc-200/90 shadow-xs'
                  : 'bg-zinc-100/70 border-zinc-200/80 text-zinc-500'
                  }`}
              >
                <div className="space-y-3">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                    <span className="text-sm font-bold text-zinc-950">
                      {slot.label}
                    </span>

                    {isOccupied ? (
                      <span
                        className={`px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full border text-[11px] font-semibold ${slot.hasReady
                          ? 'bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-200 text-emerald-800'
                          : 'bg-amber-50 border-amber-200 text-amber-800'
                          }`}
                      >
                        {slot.hasReady
                          ? 'Pronto a Servir'
                          : `${slot.orders.length} Ativo(s)`}
                      </span>
                    ) : (
                      <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-zinc-400">
                        Livre
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  {isOccupied && (
                    <div className="space-y-2.5 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200/80 bg-zinc-50 p-3">
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                        {slot.orders.map((o) => (
                          <span
                            key={o.id}
                            className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-white text-[11px] font-mono font-semibold text-zinc-900"
                          >
                            #{o.orderNumber}
                          </span>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                      {isOwner && (
                        <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between">
                          <span className="text-xs text-zinc-500">
                            Conta ativa:
                          </span>
                          <span className="text-sm font-bold font-mono text-zinc-950">
                            {slot.totalBill.toFixed(2)} â‚¬
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>

                {isOwner && isOccupied && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() =>
                      void handleCloseAllTableOrders(slot.tableNumber)
                    }
                    className="w-full py-2.5 px-3 rounded-xl border border-zinc-900 bg-zinc-900 hover:bg-zinc-800 text-white text-xs font-semibold shadow-2xs transition-all active:scale-[0.98] cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    Concluir / Fechar Mesa
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          <ConsolidatedItems orders={filteredOrders} />

          {isLoading ? (
            <div className="bg-white border border-zinc-200/90 rounded-2xl py-20 text-center shadow-xs">
              <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-700">
                A sincronizar pedidos...
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : dashboardError ? (
            <div
              role="alert"
              className="mx-auto max-w-lg rounded-2xl border border-rose-200 bg-rose-50 p-6 sm:p-8 text-center shadow-xs space-y-3"
            >
              <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-rose-950">
                NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel carregar os pedidos
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-rose-800">{dashboardError}</p>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={fetchOrders}
                className="mt-2 cursor-pointer rounded-xl border border-zinc-900 bg-zinc-900 px-5 py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold text-white shadow-2xs transition-all hover:bg-zinc-800"
              >
                Tentar Novamente
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : filteredOrders.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="mx-auto max-w-lg rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200/90 bg-white p-10 sm:p-14 text-center shadow-xs space-y-3">
              <h3 className="text-base font-bold tracking-tight text-zinc-950">
                {sectionFilter === 'all'
                  ? 'Sem pedidos ativos na bancada'
                  : `Sem pedidos ativos para ${sectionFilter === 'bar' ? 'Bar / Copa' : 'Cozinha'
                  }`}
              </h3>
              <p className="mx-auto max-w-xs text-xs leading-relaxed text-zinc-500">
                Os novos pedidos submetidos pelos clientes surgirÃ£o aqui automaticamente em tempo real.
              </p>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleManualRefresh}
                className="mt-3 cursor-pointer rounded-xl border border-zinc-900 bg-zinc-900 hover:bg-zinc-800 px-6 py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold text-white shadow-2xs transition-all active:scale-[0.98]"
              >
                Sincronizar Agora
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:gap-5 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 2xl:grid-cols-4 min-[1800px]:grid-cols-5">
              {filteredOrders.map((order) => (
                <OrderCard
                  key={order.id}
                  order={order}
                  isNew={newOrderIds.has(order.id)}
                  isUpdating={Boolean(updatingMap[order.id])}
                  isOwner={isOwner}
                  error={orderErrorMap[order.id]}
                  onClearError={() =>
                    setOrderErrorMap((previous) => ({
                      ...previous,
                      [order.id]: null,
                    }))
                  }
                  onUpdateStatus={handleUpdateStatus}
                  onPrintOrder={handlePrintOrder}
                  onChangeTable={isOwner ? handleChangeOrderTable : undefined}
                />
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </>
      )}

      {/* Undo Toast for completed orders */}
      {isOwner && undoOrder && (
        <aside
          role="status"
          aria-label="Aviso de pedido concluÃ­do com opÃ§Ã£o de desfazer"
          className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-50 flex items-center gap-4 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900 px-5 py-3.5 text-white shadow-xl animate-in slide-in-from-bottom-5 fade-in duration-200"
        >
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 text-xs sm:text-sm font-medium text-zinc-200">
            <span className="h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-400" />
            <span>
              Pedido <strong className="text-white">#{undoOrder.orderNumber}</strong> (
              {undoOrder.tableNumber === 0
                ? 'Take-Away'
                : `Mesa ${undoOrder.tableNumber}`}
              ) concluÃ­do.
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border-l border-zinc-700 pl-3">
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={isUndoing}
              onClick={handleUndo}
              className="cursor-pointer rounded-xl bg-white px-3.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-950 transition-all hover:bg-zinc-100 active:scale-[0.98] disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {isUndoing ? 'A reverter...' : 'Desfazer'}
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                if (undoTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(undoTimerRef.current);
                setUndoOrder(null);
              }}
              className="cursor-pointer rounded-xl bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-300 transition-colors hover:bg-zinc-700 hover:text-white"
              aria-label="Fechar aviso"
            >
              Fechar
            </button>
          </div>
        </aside>
      )}

      {/* Centro de QR Codes (Mesas & Take-Away) */}
      <TableQrCodesModal
        isOpen={isQrModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsQrModalOpen(false)}
        restaurantName={restaurantName}
        restaurantSlug={restaurantSlug}
      />

      {/* TalÃ£o TÃ©rmico 80mm para Impressora de Cozinha */}
      <ThermalReceipt
        order={printingOrder}
        restaurantName={restaurantName}
        isOwner={isOwner}
      />
    </div>
  );
}


========== src/components/admin/orders/OrderStatusActions.tsx ==========

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { OrderStatus } from '@/types/restaurant';
import { ValidNextStatus } from '@/lib/validations/order-management';

interface OrderStatusActionsProps {
  orderNumber: number;
  tableNumber: number;
  status: OrderStatus;
  isUpdating: boolean;
  isOwner?: boolean;
  onUpdateStatus: (nextStatus: ValidNextStatus) => Promise<void>;
}

export function OrderStatusActions({
  orderNumber,
  tableNumber,
  status,
  isUpdating,
  isOwner = true,
  onUpdateStatus,
}: OrderStatusActionsProps) {
  const [activeNextStatus, setActiveNextStatus] =
    useState<ValidNextStatus | null>(null);
  const [isConfirmingCancel, setIsConfirmingCancel] = useState(false);

  const handleAction = async (nextStatus: ValidNextStatus) => {
    if (isUpdating) return;
    try {
      setActiveNextStatus(nextStatus);
      await onUpdateStatus(nextStatus);
    } finally {
      setActiveNextStatus(null);
      setIsConfirmingCancel(false);
    }
  };

  if (status === 'completed' || status === 'cancelled') {
    return null;
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-2">
      {isConfirmingCancel ? (
        <div className="p-3 bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 rounded-xl space-y-2.5">
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-rose-900 leading-snug">
            Cancelar pedido #{orderNumber} (
            {tableNumber === 0 ? 'Take-Away' : `Mesa ${tableNumber}`})?
          </p>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={isUpdating}
              onClick={() => handleAction('cancelled')}
              className="flex-1 py-2.5 px-3 rounded-xl border border-rose-700 bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-700 text-white text-xs font-semibold shadow-2xs transition-all active:scale-[0.99] disabled:opacity-50 cursor-pointer"
            >
              {isUpdating && activeNextStatus === 'cancelled'
                ? 'A cancelar...'
                : 'Confirmar Cancelamento'}
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={isUpdating}
              onClick={() => setIsConfirmingCancel(false)}
              className="py-2.5 px-4 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white hover:bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-700 text-xs font-semibold shadow-2xs transition-all disabled:opacity-50 cursor-pointer"
            >
              Voltar
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          {status === 'pending' && (
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={isUpdating}
              onClick={() => handleAction('accepted')}
              aria-label={`Aceitar pedido nÃºmero ${orderNumber}`}
              className="flex-1 py-2.5 px-4 rounded-xl border border-zinc-900 bg-zinc-900 hover:bg-zinc-800 text-white text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold shadow-2xs transition-all active:scale-[0.99] disabled:opacity-50 cursor-pointer"
            >
              {isUpdating && activeNextStatus === 'accepted'
                ? 'A aceitar...'
                : 'Aceitar Pedido'}
            </button>
          )}

          {status === 'accepted' && (
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={isUpdating}
              onClick={() => handleAction('preparing')}
              aria-label={`Iniciar preparaÃ§Ã£o do pedido nÃºmero ${orderNumber}`}
              className="flex-1 py-2.5 px-4 rounded-xl border border-amber-700 bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-700 text-white text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold shadow-2xs transition-all active:scale-[0.99] disabled:opacity-50 cursor-pointer"
            >
              {isUpdating && activeNextStatus === 'preparing'
                ? 'A iniciar...'
                : 'Iniciar PreparaÃ§Ã£o'}
            </button>
          )}

          {status === 'preparing' && (
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={isUpdating}
              onClick={() => handleAction('ready')}
              aria-label={`Marcar pedido nÃºmero ${orderNumber} como pronto`}
              className="flex-1 py-2.5 px-4 rounded-xl border border-emerald-700 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold shadow-2xs transition-all active:scale-[0.99] disabled:opacity-50 cursor-pointer"
            >
              {isUpdating && activeNextStatus === 'ready'
                ? 'A atualizar...'
                : 'Pronto p/ Entrega'}
            </button>
          )}

          {status === 'ready' && (
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={isUpdating}
              onClick={() => handleAction('completed')}
              aria-label={`Concluir entrega do pedido nÃºmero ${orderNumber}`}
              className="flex-1 py-2.5 px-4 rounded-xl border border-zinc-900 bg-zinc-900 hover:bg-zinc-800 text-white text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold shadow-2xs transition-all active:scale-[0.99] disabled:opacity-50 cursor-pointer"
            >
              {isUpdating && activeNextStatus === 'completed'
                ? 'A concluir...'
                : 'Concluir / Entregue'}
            </button>
          )}

          {isOwner && (
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={isUpdating}
              onClick={() => setIsConfirmingCancel(true)}
              aria-label={`Cancelar pedido nÃºmero ${orderNumber}`}
              title="Cancelar Pedido"
              className="py-2.5 px-3.5 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200/90 bg-white hover:bg-rose-600 hover:border-rose-600 text-zinc-600 hover:text-white text-xs font-semibold shadow-2xs transition-all active:scale-[0.99] disabled:opacity-50 cursor-pointer shrink-0"
            >
              Cancelar
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.create_order_atomic(
    p_restaurant_slug TEXT,
    p_table_number INTEGER,
    p_customer_notes TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
    p_items JSONB DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
    -- VariÃ¡veis do Restaurante
    v_restaurant RECORD;
    -- VariÃ¡veis da Mesa
    v_table RECORD;
    -- VariÃ¡veis de Processamento de Pedido
    v_order_id UUID;
    v_order_number INTEGER;
    v_created_at TIMESTAMPTZ;
    v_tracking_token VARCHAR(64);
    v_subtotal NUMERIC(10,2) := 0.00;
    v_total NUMERIC(10,2) := 0.00;
    -- IteraÃ§Ã£o de Itens
    v_item JSONB;
    v_product_id_text TEXT;
    v_product_id UUID;
    v_quantity INTEGER;
    v_item_notes TEXT;
    v_product RECORD;
    v_order_item_id UUID;
    -- Arrays para retorno estruturado
    v_returned_items JSONB := '[]'::jsonb;
    v_item_response JSONB;
    -- Tabela temporÃ¡ria / array em memÃ³ria de validaÃ§Ã£o de itens
    v_items_count INTEGER;
BEGIN
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 1. VALIDAÃ‡ÃƒO DO RESTAURANTE (Zero-Trust)
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    IF p_restaurant_slug IS NULL OR trim(p_restaurant_slug) = '' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'RESTAURANT_NOT_FOUND: Slug do restaurante nÃ£o fornecido.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    SELECT id, name, slug, is_active, is_open
    INTO v_restaurant
    FROM public.restaurants
    WHERE slug = trim(p_restaurant_slug);

    IF NOT FOUND THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'RESTAURANT_NOT_FOUND: Restaurante com o slug "%" nÃ£o foi encontrado.', p_restaurant_slug USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    IF NOT v_restaurant.is_active THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'RESTAURANT_INACTIVE: O restaurante "%" encontra-se inativo no sistema.', v_restaurant.name USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    IF NOT v_restaurant.is_open THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'RESTAURANT_CLOSED: O restaurante "%" encontra-se encerrado de momento.', v_restaurant.name USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 2. VALIDAÃ‡ÃƒO DA MESA
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    IF p_table_number IS NULL OR p_table_number <= 0 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'TABLE_INVALID: NÃºmero da mesa invÃ¡lido.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    SELECT id, number, name, is_active
    INTO v_table
    FROM public.tables
    WHERE restaurant_id = v_restaurant.id
      AND number = p_table_number;

    IF NOT FOUND THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'TABLE_INVALID: A Mesa % nÃ£o existe no restaurante %.', p_table_number, v_restaurant.name USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    IF NOT v_table.is_active THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'TABLE_INVALID: A Mesa % encontra-se desativada de momento.', p_table_number USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 3. VALIDAÃ‡ÃƒO DE OBSERVAÃ‡Ã•ES GERAIS
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    IF p_customer_notes IS NOT NULL AND length(trim(p_customer_notes)) > 1000 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'INVALID_NOTES: As observaÃ§Ãµes gerais excedem o limite de 1000 caracteres.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 4. VALIDAÃ‡ÃƒO DO CARRINHO (Estrutura e Limites)
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    IF p_items IS NULL OR jsonb_typeof(p_items) <> 'array' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'EMPTY_CART: Formato de artigos invÃ¡lido.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    v_items_count := jsonb_array_length(p_items);
    IF v_items_count = 0 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'EMPTY_CART: O carrinho estÃ¡ vazio.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    IF v_items_count > 50 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'INVALID_QUANTITY: O pedido nÃ£o pode conter mais de 50 artigos distintos.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 5. CRIAÃ‡ÃƒO DE TABELA TEMPORÃRIA PARA ISOLAMENTO E CÃLCULO SEGURO
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    CREATE TEMPORARY TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _order_staging_items (
        product_id UUID NOT NULL,
        product_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
        quantity INTEGER NOT NULL,
        unit_price NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL,
        unit_cost NUMERIC(10,2),
        notes TEXT
    ) ON COMMIT DROP;

    TRUNCATE TABLE _order_staging_items;

    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 6. VALIDAÃ‡ÃƒO DOS ITENS E OBTENÃ‡ÃƒO DE PREÃ‡OS REAIS DA BD
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    FOR v_item IN SELECT * FROM jsonb_array_elements(p_items)
    LOOP
        v_product_id_text := v_item->>'product_id';
        
        -- Validar formato UUID
        IF v_product_id_text IS NULL OR v_product_id_text !~* '^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$' THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'PRODUCT_INVALID: Formato de identificador de produto invÃ¡lido.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END IF;

        v_product_id := v_product_id_text::UUID;

        -- Validar quantidade (1 a 50)
        BEGIN
            v_quantity := (v_item->>'quantity')::INTEGER;
        EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'INVALID_QUANTITY: Quantidade deve ser um nÃºmero inteiro vÃ¡lido.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END;

        IF v_quantity IS NULL OR v_quantity < 1 OR v_quantity > 50 THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'INVALID_QUANTITY: A quantidade por artigo deve estar entre 1 e 50.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END IF;

        -- Validar notas do item (mÃ¡x 500 caracteres)
        v_item_notes := nullif(trim(v_item->>'notes'), '');
        IF v_item_notes IS NOT NULL AND length(v_item_notes) > 500 THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'INVALID_NOTES: A observaÃ§Ã£o do artigo excede o limite de 500 caracteres.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END IF;

        -- Procurar produto ativo e categoria ativa no restaurante correto
        SELECT 
            p.id,
            p.name,
            p.price,
            p.cost,
            p.is_available,
            c.is_active AS category_is_active
        INTO v_product
        FROM public.products p
        JOIN public.categories c ON c.id = p.category_id
        WHERE p.id = v_product_id
          AND p.restaurant_id = v_restaurant.id
          AND c.restaurant_id = v_restaurant.id;

        IF NOT FOUND THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'PRODUCT_INVALID: O artigo selecionado nÃ£o existe ou nÃ£o pertence ao restaurante %.', v_restaurant.name USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END IF;

        IF NOT v_product.is_available THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'PRODUCT_UNAVAILABLE: O artigo "%" encontra-se esgotado / indisponÃ­vel.', v_product.name USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END IF;

        IF NOT v_product.category_is_active THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'PRODUCT_UNAVAILABLE: A categoria do artigo "%" encontra-se desativada de momento.', v_product.name USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END IF;

        -- SomatÃ³rio com precisÃ£o NUMERIC da BD
        v_subtotal := v_subtotal + (v_product.price * v_quantity);

        -- Guardar item validado no staging
        INSERT INTO _order_staging_items (
            product_id,
            product_name,
            quantity,
            unit_price,
            unit_cost,
            notes
        ) VALUES (
            v_product.id,
            v_product.name,
            v_quantity,
            v_product.price,
            v_product.cost,
            v_item_notes
        );
    END LOOP;

    -- No M3: total = subtotal
    v_total := v_subtotal;

    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 7. GERAÃ‡ÃƒO DO ORDER NUMBER ATÃ“MICO
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    v_order_number := public.get_next_order_number(v_restaurant.id);

    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 8. INSERÃ‡ÃƒO DO PEDIDO (ORDERS)
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    INSERT INTO public.orders (
        restaurant_id,
        table_id,
        table_number,
        order_number,
        status,
        subtotal,
        total,
        customer_notes
    ) VALUES (
        v_restaurant.id,
        v_table.id,
        v_table.number,
        v_order_number,
        'pending',
        v_subtotal,
        v_total,
        nullif(trim(p_customer_notes), '')
    )
    RETURNING id, created_at, tracking_token INTO v_order_id, v_created_at, v_tracking_token;

    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 9. INSERÃ‡ÃƒO DOS ITENS (ORDER_ITEMS) COM SNAPSHOT HISTÃ“RICO
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    FOR v_product IN 
        SELECT product_id, product_name, quantity, unit_price, unit_cost, notes 
        FROM _order_staging_items
    LOOP
        INSERT INTO public.order_items (
            order_id,
            product_id,
            product_name,
            quantity,
            unit_price,
            unit_cost,
            notes
        ) VALUES (
            v_order_id,
            v_product.product_id,
            v_product.product_name,
            v_product.quantity,
            v_product.unit_price,
            v_product.unit_cost,
            v_product.notes
        )
        RETURNING id INTO v_order_item_id;

        -- Construir item para resposta JSON
        v_item_response := jsonb_build_object(
            'id', v_order_item_id,
            'product_id', v_product.product_id,
            'product_name', v_product.product_name,
            'quantity', v_product.quantity,
            'unit_price', v_product.unit_price,
            'notes', v_product.notes
        );

        v_returned_items := v_returned_items || jsonb_build_array(v_item_response);
    END LOOP;

    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 10. RETORNO ESTRUTURADO PARA O FRONTEND / CAMADA NEXT.JS
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
        'success', true,
        'order', jsonb_build_object(
            'id', v_order_id,
            'restaurant_id', v_restaurant.id,
            'restaurant_slug', v_restaurant.slug,
            'restaurant_name', v_restaurant.name,
            'table_id', v_table.id,
            'table_number', v_table.number,
            'table_name', v_table.name,
            'order_number', v_order_number,
            'status', 'pending',
            'subtotal', v_subtotal,
            'total', v_total,
            'customer_notes', nullif(trim(p_customer_notes), ''),
            'created_at', v_created_at,
            'tracking_token', v_tracking_token,
            'items', v_returned_items
        )
    );
END;
$$;

-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- PERMISSÃ•ES DE EXECUÃ‡ÃƒO
-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Revoga privilÃ©gios pÃºblicos padrÃ£o e concede apenas EXECUTE aos roles necessÃ¡rios.
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.create_order_atomic(TEXT, INTEGER, TEXT, JSONB) FROM PUBLIC;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.create_order_atomic(TEXT, INTEGER, TEXT, JSONB) TO anon, authenticated, service_role;

COMMENT ON FUNCTION public.create_order_atomic IS 'CriaÃ§Ã£o transacional e atÃ³mica de pedidos no Gulas com validaÃ§Ã£o server-side de preÃ§os, restaurante, mesa e produtos.';

-- FunÃ§Ã£o atÃ³mica protegida contra concorrÃªncia para gerar nÃºmeros de pedido
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.get_next_order_number(p_restaurant_id UUID)
RETURNS INTEGER AS $$
DECLARE
    v_next_number INTEGER;
BEGIN
    INSERT INTO public.restaurant_counters (restaurant_id, last_order_number, updated_at)
    VALUES (p_restaurant_id, 1, NOW())
    ON CONFLICT (restaurant_id) DO UPDATE
    SET last_order_number = public.restaurant_counters.last_order_number + 1,
        updated_at = NOW()
    RETURNING last_order_number INTO v_next_number;

    RETURN v_next_number;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public;

-- ============================================================================
-- MigraÃ§Ã£o M4.4: Habilitar Supabase Realtime para KDS
-- Adiciona as tabelas 'orders' e 'order_items' Ã  publicaÃ§Ã£o 'supabase_realtime'
-- e define REPLICA IDENTITY FULL para suporte a filtros em UPDATE/DELETE
-- ============================================================================

DO $$
BEGIN
    -- Adicionar public.orders se ainda nÃ£o estiver na publicaÃ§Ã£o
    IF NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM pg_publication_rel pr
        JOIN pg_class c ON c.oid = pr.prrelid
        JOIN pg_publication p ON p.oid = pr.prpubid
        WHERE p.pubname = 'supabase_realtime'
        AND c.relname = 'orders'
    ) THEN
        ALTER PUBLICATION supabase_realtime ADD TABLE public.orders;
    END IF;

    -- Adicionar public.order_items se ainda nÃ£o estiver na publicaÃ§Ã£o
    IF NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM pg_publication_rel pr
        JOIN pg_class c ON c.oid = pr.prrelid
        JOIN pg_publication p ON p.oid = pr.prpubid
        WHERE p.pubname = 'supabase_realtime'
        AND c.relname = 'order_items'
    ) THEN
        ALTER PUBLICATION supabase_realtime ADD TABLE public.order_items;
    END IF;
END $$;

-- Garante que o WAL do PostgreSQL contenha todas as colunas da linha anterior
-- para permitir avaliaÃ§Ã£o de RLS e filtros em eventos de UPDATE e DELETE
ALTER TABLE public.orders REPLICA IDENTITY FULL;

-- ============================================================================
-- MigraÃ§Ã£o: Permitir Leitura PÃºblica de Pedidos para Live Order Tracking
-- Permite que clientes consultem o estado do seu pedido e subscrevam eventos
-- Realtime atravÃ©s da tabela public.orders e public.order_items
-- ============================================================================

-- Ativa polÃ­ticas de SELECT para anon e authenticated em orders
DO $$
BEGIN
    IF NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_policies
        WHERE tablename = 'orders'
        AND policyname = 'Public Read Orders'
    ) THEN
        CREATE POLICY "Public Read Orders"
        ON public.orders FOR SELECT
        TO anon, authenticated
        USING (true);
    END IF;

    IF NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_policies
        WHERE tablename = 'order_items'
        AND policyname = 'Public Read Order Items'
    ) THEN
        CREATE POLICY "Public Read Order Items"
        ON public.order_items FOR SELECT
        TO anon, authenticated
        USING (true);
    END IF;
END $$;

-- Impede que um utilizador comum autenticado altere o seu restaurant_id ou
-- se promova a "owner" via API de cliente Supabase.
-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.protect_profile_privileges()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF (OLD.restaurant_id IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.restaurant_id) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'VIOLATION_TENANT: NÃ£o Ã© permitido alterar o restaurante associado ao perfil.' USING ERRCODE = '42501';
    END IF;
    IF (OLD.role IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.role) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'VIOLATION_ROLE: NÃ£o Ã© permitido alterar o cargo/papel atribuÃ­do ao perfil.' USING ERRCODE = '42501';
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public;

    BEFORE UPDATE ON public.profiles

-- propriedade/autorizaÃ§Ã£o real pelo cliente que realizou o pedido.

ALTER TABLE public.orders 
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS tracking_token VARCHAR(64) DEFAULT encode(gen_random_bytes(32), 'hex');

-- RPC protegida que exige ID e tracking_token vÃ¡lido (ou sessÃ£o de staff do restaurante)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.get_public_order_status(
    p_order_id UUID,
    p_tracking_token TEXT DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_result JSONB;
    v_is_staff BOOLEAN := false;
BEGIN
    IF p_order_id IS NULL THEN
        RETURN NULL;
    END IF;

    -- Verificar se Ã© staff autenticado do prÃ³prio restaurante
    IF auth.uid() IS NOT NULL THEN
        SELECT EXISTS (
            SELECT 1 FROM public.orders o
            JOIN public.profiles p ON p.restaurant_id = o.restaurant_id
            WHERE o.id = p_order_id AND p.id = auth.uid()
        ) INTO v_is_staff;
    END IF;

    -- Se nÃ£o for staff do restaurante, exige o tracking_token correspondente
    IF NOT v_is_staff THEN
        IF p_tracking_token IS NULL OR trim(p_tracking_token) = '' THEN
            RETURN NULL;
        END IF;

        IF NOT EXISTS (
            SELECT 1 FROM public.orders 
            WHERE id = p_order_id AND tracking_token = trim(p_tracking_token)
        ) THEN
            RETURN NULL;
        END IF;
    END IF;

    -- ProjeÃ§Ã£o estrita dos dados de acompanhamento (sem custos internos)
    SELECT jsonb_build_object(
        'id', o.id,
        'order_number', o.order_number,
        'table_number', o.table_number,
        'status', o.status,
        'subtotal', o.subtotal,
        'total', o.total,
        'created_at', o.created_at,
        'updated_at', o.updated_at,
        'items', COALESCE(
            (
                SELECT jsonb_agg(
                    jsonb_build_object(
                        'id', oi.id,
                        'product_name', oi.product_name,
                        'quantity', oi.quantity,
                        'unit_price', oi.unit_price,
                        'notes', oi.notes
                    )
                )
                FROM public.order_items oi
                WHERE oi.order_id = o.id
            ),
            '[]'::jsonb
        )
    )
    INTO v_result
    FROM public.orders o
    WHERE o.id = p_order_id;

    RETURN v_result;
END;
$$;

REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.get_public_order_status(UUID, TEXT) FROM PUBLIC;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.get_public_order_status(UUID, TEXT) TO anon, authenticated;

-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 6. PROTEÃ‡ÃƒO DE CUSTOS DE PRODUTOS (COLUMN-LEVEL SECURITY)
-- Impede que anÃ³nimos ou staff de outros restaurantes leiam a coluna `cost`
-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
REVOKE ALL ON public.products FROM anon;
GRANT ALL ON public.products TO authenticated;
GRANT SELECT (
    id,
    restaurant_id,
    category_id,
    name,
    slug,
    description,
    price,
    image_url,
    is_available,
    display_order,
    badges,
    unit_quantity,
    includes_notes,
    customization_note,
    allergens,
    prep_time_minutes,
    created_at,
    updated_at
) ON public.products TO anon, authenticated;

COMMIT;

========== C:\Users\hugom\.antigravity-ide\gulas\supabase\migrations\20261017_restrict_order_item_cost.sql ==========

-- ============================================================================
-- GULAS: restricao do custo unitario dos itens de pedido
-- Data: 2026-10-17
--
-- Este ficheiro:
-- 1. Retira a leitura direta de order_items aos clientes.
-- 2. Repoe apenas as colunas necessarias para a experiencia publica.
-- 3. Valida que unit_cost deixou de estar acessivel.
--
-- Nao elimina dados e nao altera politicas RLS.
-- ============================================================================


BEGIN;


REVOKE SELECT ON TABLE public.order_items
FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;


GRANT SELECT (
    id,
    order_id,
    product_id,
    product_name,
    quantity,
    unit_price,
    notes,
    created_at
)
ON TABLE public.order_items
TO anon, authenticated;


DO $$
BEGIN
    IF pg_catalog.has_column_privilege(
        'anon',
        'public.order_items',
        'unit_cost',
        'SELECT'
    ) OR pg_catalog.has_column_privilege(
        'authenticated',
        'public.order_items',
        'unit_cost',
        'SELECT'
    ) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION
            'Validacao falhou: unit_cost continua acessivel aos clientes.';
    END IF;
END;
$$;


COMMIT;

========== C:\Users\hugom\.antigravity-ide\gulas\supabase\migrations\20261017_restrict_product_cost_and_internal_rpc.sql ==========

-- ============================================================================
-- GULAS: restricao de custos e de RPC interna
-- Data: 2026-10-17
--
-- Pre-requisito:
-- A estrutura e as funcoes da migration de seguranca anterior devem existir.
--
-- Este ficheiro:
-- 1. Limita a leitura direta de products a colunas publicas.
-- 2. Retira aos clientes a execucao direta da numeracao de pedidos.
-- 3. Fixa o search_path de duas funcoes previamente revistas.
--
-- Nao elimina dados e nao altera o corpo de create_order_atomic.
-- ============================================================================

BEGIN;

-- 1. Retirar SELECT geral e repor apenas colunas publicas.
REVOKE SELECT ON TABLE public.products
FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;

GRANT SELECT (
    id,
    restaurant_id,
    category_id,
    name,
    slug,
    description,
    price,
    image_url,
    is_available,
    display_order,
    badges,
    unit_quantity,
    includes_notes,
    customization_note,
    allergens,
    prep_time_minutes,
    created_at,
    updated_at
)
ON TABLE public.products
TO anon, authenticated;

-- 2. A numeracao deve ser chamada internamente pela criacao do pedido.
REVOKE EXECUTE
ON FUNCTION public.get_next_order_number(UUID)
FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;

-- 3. Ajustar apenas funcoes cujo corpo ja foi inspecionado.
ALTER FUNCTION public.get_public_order_status(UUID, TEXT)
SET search_path = pg_catalog, public, pg_temp;

ALTER FUNCTION public.protect_profile_privileges()
SET search_path = pg_catalog, public, pg_temp;

-- 4. Validar as restricoes antes de concluir a transacao.
DO $$
BEGIN
    IF pg_catalog.has_column_privilege(
        'anon',
        'public.products',
        'cost',
        'SELECT'
    ) OR pg_catalog.has_column_privilege(
        'authenticated',
        'public.products',
        'cost',
        'SELECT'
    ) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION
            'Validacao falhou: cost continua acessivel aos clientes.';
    END IF;

    IF pg_catalog.has_function_privilege(
        'anon',
        'public.get_next_order_number(uuid)',
        'EXECUTE'
    ) OR pg_catalog.has_function_privilege(
        'authenticated',
        'public.get_next_order_number(uuid)',
        'EXECUTE'
    ) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION
            'Validacao falhou: numeracao continua acessivel aos clientes.';
    END IF;
END;
$$;

COMMIT;
