    v_product RECORD;
    v_order_item_id UUID;
    -- Arrays para retorno estruturado
    v_returned_items JSONB := '[]'::jsonb;
    v_item_response JSONB;
    -- Tabela temporÃ¡ria / array em memÃ³ria de validaÃ§Ã£o de itens
    v_items_count INTEGER;
BEGIN
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 1. VALIDAÃ‡ÃƒO DO RESTAURANTE (Zero-Trust)
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    IF p_restaurant_slug IS NULL OR trim(p_restaurant_slug) = '' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'RESTAURANT_NOT_FOUND: Slug do restaurante nÃ£o fornecido.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    SELECT id, name, slug, is_active, is_open
    INTO v_restaurant
    FROM public.restaurants
    WHERE slug = trim(p_restaurant_slug);

    IF NOT FOUND THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'RESTAURANT_NOT_FOUND: Restaurante com o slug "%" nÃ£o foi encontrado.', p_restaurant_slug USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    IF NOT v_restaurant.is_active THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'RESTAURANT_INACTIVE: O restaurante "%" encontra-se inativo no sistema.', v_restaurant.name USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    IF NOT v_restaurant.is_open THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'RESTAURANT_CLOSED: O restaurante "%" encontra-se encerrado de momento.', v_restaurant.name USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 2. VALIDAÃ‡ÃƒO DA MESA
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    IF p_table_number IS NULL OR p_table_number <= 0 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'TABLE_INVALID: NÃºmero da mesa invÃ¡lido.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    SELECT id, number, name, is_active
    INTO v_table
    FROM public.tables
    WHERE restaurant_id = v_restaurant.id
      AND number = p_table_number;

    IF NOT FOUND THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'TABLE_INVALID: A Mesa % nÃ£o existe no restaurante %.', p_table_number, v_restaurant.name USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    IF NOT v_table.is_active THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'TABLE_INVALID: A Mesa % encontra-se desativada de momento.', p_table_number USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 3. VALIDAÃ‡ÃƒO DE OBSERVAÃ‡Ã•ES GERAIS
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    IF p_customer_notes IS NOT NULL AND length(trim(p_customer_notes)) > 1000 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'INVALID_NOTES: As observaÃ§Ãµes gerais excedem o limite de 1000 caracteres.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 4. VALIDAÃ‡ÃƒO DO CARRINHO (Estrutura e Limites)
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    IF p_items IS NULL OR jsonb_typeof(p_items) <> 'array' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'EMPTY_CART: Formato de artigos invÃ¡lido.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    v_items_count := jsonb_array_length(p_items);
    IF v_items_count = 0 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'EMPTY_CART: O carrinho estÃ¡ vazio.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    IF v_items_count > 50 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'INVALID_QUANTITY: O pedido nÃ£o pode conter mais de 50 artigos distintos.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 5. CRIAÃ‡ÃƒO DE TABELA TEMPORÃRIA PARA ISOLAMENTO E CÃLCULO SEGURO
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    CREATE TEMPORARY TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _order_staging_items (
        product_id UUID NOT NULL,
        product_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
        quantity INTEGER NOT NULL,
        unit_price NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL,
        unit_cost NUMERIC(10,2),
        notes TEXT
    ) ON COMMIT DROP;

    TRUNCATE TABLE _order_staging_items;

    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 6. VALIDAÃ‡ÃƒO DOS ITENS E OBTENÃ‡ÃƒO DE PREÃ‡OS REAIS DA BD
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    FOR v_item IN SELECT * FROM jsonb_array_elements(p_items)
    LOOP
        v_product_id_text := v_item->>'product_id';
        
        -- Validar formato UUID
        IF v_product_id_text IS NULL OR v_product_id_text !~* '^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$' THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'PRODUCT_INVALID: Formato de identificador de produto invÃ¡lido.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END IF;

        v_product_id := v_product_id_text::UUID;

        -- Validar quantidade (1 a 50)
        BEGIN
            v_quantity := (v_item->>'quantity')::INTEGER;
        EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'INVALID_QUANTITY: Quantidade deve ser um nÃºmero inteiro vÃ¡lido.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END;

        IF v_quantity IS NULL OR v_quantity < 1 OR v_quantity > 50 THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'INVALID_QUANTITY: A quantidade por artigo deve estar entre 1 e 50.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END IF;

        -- Validar notas do item (mÃ¡x 500 caracteres)
        v_item_notes := nullif(trim(v_item->>'notes'), '');
        IF v_item_notes IS NOT NULL AND length(v_item_notes) > 500 THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'INVALID_NOTES: A observaÃ§Ã£o do artigo excede o limite de 500 caracteres.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END IF;

        -- Procurar produto ativo e categoria ativa no restaurante correto
        SELECT 
            p.id,
            p.name,
            p.price,
            p.cost,
            p.is_available,
            c.is_active AS category_is_active
        INTO v_product
        FROM public.products p
        JOIN public.categories c ON c.id = p.category_id
        WHERE p.id = v_product_id
          AND p.restaurant_id = v_restaurant.id
          AND c.restaurant_id = v_restaurant.id;

        IF NOT FOUND THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'PRODUCT_INVALID: O artigo selecionado nÃ£o existe ou nÃ£o pertence ao restaurante %.', v_restaurant.name USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END IF;

        IF NOT v_product.is_available THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'PRODUCT_UNAVAILABLE: O artigo "%" encontra-se esgotado / indisponÃ­vel.', v_product.name USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END IF;

        IF NOT v_product.category_is_active THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'PRODUCT_UNAVAILABLE: A categoria do artigo "%" encontra-se desativada de momento.', v_product.name USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END IF;

        -- SomatÃ³rio com precisÃ£o NUMERIC da BD
        v_subtotal := v_subtotal + (v_product.price * v_quantity);

        -- Guardar item validado no staging
        INSERT INTO _order_staging_items (
            product_id,
            product_name,
            quantity,
            unit_price,
            unit_cost,
            notes
        ) VALUES (
            v_product.id,
            v_product.name,
            v_quantity,
            v_product.price,
            v_product.cost,
            v_item_notes
        );
    END LOOP;

    -- No M3: total = subtotal
    v_total := v_subtotal;

    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 7. GERAÃ‡ÃƒO DO ORDER NUMBER ATÃ“MICO
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    v_order_number := public.get_next_order_number(v_restaurant.id);

    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 8. INSERÃ‡ÃƒO DO PEDIDO (ORDERS) COM RETORNO DE TRACKING_TOKEN
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    INSERT INTO public.orders (
        restaurant_id,
        table_id,
        table_number,
        order_number,
        status,
        subtotal,
        total,
        customer_notes
    ) VALUES (
        v_restaurant.id,
        v_table.id,
        v_table.number,
        v_order_number,
        'pending',
        v_subtotal,
        v_total,
        nullif(trim(p_customer_notes), '')
    )
    RETURNING id, created_at, tracking_token INTO v_order_id, v_created_at, v_tracking_token;

    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 9. INSERÃ‡ÃƒO DOS ITENS (ORDER_ITEMS) COM SNAPSHOT HISTÃ“RICO
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    FOR v_product IN 
        SELECT product_id, product_name, quantity, unit_price, unit_cost, notes 
        FROM _order_staging_items
    LOOP
        INSERT INTO public.order_items (
            order_id,
            product_id,
            product_name,
            quantity,
            unit_price,
            unit_cost,
            notes
        ) VALUES (
            v_order_id,
            v_product.product_id,
            v_product.product_name,
            v_product.quantity,
            v_product.unit_price,
            v_product.unit_cost,
            v_product.notes
        )
        RETURNING id INTO v_order_item_id;

        -- Construir item para resposta JSON
        v_item_response := jsonb_build_object(
            'id', v_order_item_id,
            'product_id', v_product.product_id,
            'product_name', v_product.product_name,
            'quantity', v_product.quantity,
            'unit_price', v_product.unit_price,
            'notes', v_product.notes
        );

        v_returned_items := v_returned_items || jsonb_build_array(v_item_response);
    END LOOP;

    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 10. RETORNO ESTRUTURADO PARA O FRONTEND / CAMADA NEXT.JS
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
        'success', true,
        'order', jsonb_build_object(
            'id', v_order_id,
            'restaurant_id', v_restaurant.id,
            'restaurant_slug', v_restaurant.slug,
            'restaurant_name', v_restaurant.name,
            'table_id', v_table.id,
            'table_number', v_table.number,
            'table_name', v_table.name,
            'order_number', v_order_number,
            'status', 'pending',
            'subtotal', v_subtotal,
            'total', v_total,
            'customer_notes', nullif(trim(p_customer_notes), ''),
            'created_at', v_created_at,
            'tracking_token', v_tracking_token,
            'items', v_returned_items
        )
    );
END;
$$;

-- PermissÃµes de execuÃ§Ã£o
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.create_order_atomic(TEXT, INTEGER, TEXT, JSONB) FROM PUBLIC;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.create_order_atomic(TEXT, INTEGER, TEXT, JSONB) TO anon, authenticated, service_role;

COMMENT ON FUNCTION public.create_order_atomic IS 'CriaÃ§Ã£o transacional e atÃ³mica de pedidos no Gulas com validaÃ§Ã£o server-side de preÃ§os, restaurante, mesa, produtos e retorno do tracking_token.';

-- ============================================================================

BEGIN;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.create_order_atomic(
    p_restaurant_slug TEXT,
    p_table_number INTEGER,
    p_customer_notes TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
    p_items JSONB DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public, extensions
AS $$

    -- VariÃ¡veis do Restaurante
    v_restaurant RECORD;
    -- VariÃ¡veis da Mesa
    v_table RECORD;
    v_table_id UUID := NULL;
    v_table_name TEXT := 'Take-away';
    -- VariÃ¡veis de Processamento de Pedido
    v_order_id UUID;
    v_order_number INTEGER;
    v_created_at TIMESTAMPTZ;
    v_tracking_token VARCHAR(64);
    v_subtotal NUMERIC(10,2) := 0.00;
    v_total NUMERIC(10,2) := 0.00;
    -- IteraÃ§Ã£o de Itens
    v_item JSONB;
    v_product_id_text TEXT;
    v_product_id UUID;
    v_quantity INTEGER;
    v_item_notes TEXT;
    v_product RECORD;
    v_order_item_id UUID;
    -- Arrays para retorno estruturado
    v_returned_items JSONB := '[]'::jsonb;
    v_item_response JSONB;
    v_items_count INTEGER;
BEGIN
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 1. VALIDAÃ‡ÃƒO DO RESTAURANTE (Zero-Trust)
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    IF p_restaurant_slug IS NULL OR trim(p_restaurant_slug) = '' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'RESTAURANT_NOT_FOUND: Slug do restaurante nÃ£o fornecido.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    SELECT id, name, slug, is_active, is_open
    INTO v_restaurant
    FROM public.restaurants
    WHERE slug = trim(p_restaurant_slug);

    IF NOT FOUND THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'RESTAURANT_NOT_FOUND: Restaurante com o slug "%" nÃ£o foi encontrado.', p_restaurant_slug USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    IF NOT v_restaurant.is_active THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'RESTAURANT_INACTIVE: O restaurante "%" encontra-se inativo no sistema.', v_restaurant.name USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    IF NOT v_restaurant.is_open THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'RESTAURANT_CLOSED: O restaurante "%" encontra-se encerrado de momento.', v_restaurant.name USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 2. VALIDAÃ‡ÃƒO DA MESA (Mesa >= 1 = Sala, Mesa 0 = Take-away)
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    IF p_table_number IS NULL OR p_table_number < 0 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'TABLE_INVALID: NÃºmero da mesa invÃ¡lido.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    IF p_table_number = 0 THEN
        -- Pedido para Levar / Take-away: sem mesa fÃ­sica
        v_table_id := NULL;
        v_table_name := 'Take-away';
    ELSE
        SELECT id, number, name, is_active
        INTO v_table
        FROM public.tables
        WHERE restaurant_id = v_restaurant.id
          AND number = p_table_number;

        IF NOT FOUND THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'TABLE_INVALID: A Mesa % nÃ£o existe no restaurante %.', p_table_number, v_restaurant.name USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END IF;

        END IF;

        v_table_id := v_table.id;
        v_table_name := v_table.name;
    END IF;

    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 3. VALIDAÃ‡ÃƒO DE OBSERVAÃ‡Ã•ES GERAIS
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    IF p_customer_notes IS NOT NULL AND length(trim(p_customer_notes)) > 1000 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'INVALID_NOTES: As observaÃ§Ãµes gerais excedem o limite de 1000 caracteres.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 4. VALIDAÃ‡ÃƒO DO CARRINHO (Estrutura e Limites)
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    IF p_items IS NULL OR jsonb_typeof(p_items) <> 'array' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'EMPTY_CART: Formato de artigos invÃ¡lido.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    v_items_count := jsonb_array_length(p_items);
    IF v_items_count = 0 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'EMPTY_CART: O carrinho estÃ¡ vazio.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    IF v_items_count > 50 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'CART_LIMIT_EXCEEDED: Limite mÃ¡ximo de 50 artigos distintos por pedido excedido.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 5. CÃLCULO ATÃ“MICO E VALIDAÃ‡ÃƒO DOS PRODUTOS
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    FOR v_item IN SELECT * FROM jsonb_array_elements(p_items)
    LOOP
        v_product_id_text := v_item->>'product_id';
        v_quantity := (v_item->>'quantity')::INTEGER;
        v_item_notes := v_item->>'notes';

        IF v_product_id_text IS NULL THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'PRODUCT_INVALID: Identificador de artigo ausente.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END IF;

        BEGIN
            v_product_id := v_product_id_text::UUID;
        EXCEPTION WHEN invalid_text_representation THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'PRODUCT_INVALID: Formato de UUID do artigo invÃ¡lido.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END;

        IF v_quantity IS NULL OR v_quantity <= 0 OR v_quantity > 50 THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'INVALID_QUANTITY: A quantidade do artigo deve estar entre 1 e 50.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END IF;

        SELECT id, name, price, cost, is_available
        INTO v_product
        FROM public.products
        WHERE id = v_product_id
          AND restaurant_id = v_restaurant.id;

        IF NOT FOUND THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'PRODUCT_INVALID: Artigo nÃ£o encontrado no menu do restaurante.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END IF;

        IF NOT v_product.is_available THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'PRODUCT_UNAVAILABLE: O artigo "%" nÃ£o se encontra disponÃ­vel de momento.', v_product.name USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END IF;

        v_subtotal := v_subtotal + (v_product.price * v_quantity);
    END LOOP;

    v_total := v_subtotal;

    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 6. INCREMENTO ATÃ“MICO DO NÃšMERO DO PEDIDO (Sequencial por Restaurante)
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    INSERT INTO public.restaurant_counters (restaurant_id, last_order_number, updated_at)
    VALUES (v_restaurant.id, 1, NOW())
    ON CONFLICT (restaurant_id)
    DO UPDATE SET
        last_order_number = public.restaurant_counters.last_order_number + 1,
        updated_at = NOW()
    RETURNING last_order_number INTO v_order_number;

    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 7. GERAÃ‡ÃƒO DO TOKEN DE RASTREIO E INSERÃ‡ÃƒO DO PEDIDO
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    v_tracking_token := replace(gen_random_uuid()::text, '-', '') || replace(gen_random_uuid()::text, '-', '');

    INSERT INTO public.orders (
        restaurant_id,
        table_id,
        table_number,
        order_number,
        tracking_token,
        status,
        subtotal,
        total,
        customer_notes
    ) VALUES (
        v_restaurant.id,
        v_table_id,
        p_table_number,
        v_order_number,
        v_tracking_token,
        'pending',
        v_subtotal,
        v_total,
        p_customer_notes
    ) RETURNING id, created_at, tracking_token INTO v_order_id, v_created_at, v_tracking_token;

    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 8. INSERÃ‡ÃƒO DOS ITENS DO PEDIDO (Snapshots ImutÃ¡veis com PreÃ§os de Tabela)
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    FOR v_item IN SELECT * FROM jsonb_array_elements(p_items)
    LOOP
        v_product_id := (v_item->>'product_id')::UUID;
        v_quantity := (v_item->>'quantity')::INTEGER;
        v_item_notes := v_item->>'notes';

        SELECT id, name, price, cost
        INTO v_product
        FROM public.products
        WHERE id = v_product_id;

        INSERT INTO public.order_items (
            order_id,
            product_id,
            product_name,
            quantity,
            unit_price,
            unit_cost,
            notes

            v_order_id,
            v_product.id,
            v_product.name,
            v_quantity,
            v_product.price,
            v_product.cost,
            v_item_notes
        ) RETURNING id INTO v_order_item_id;

            'product_id', v_product.id,
            'product_name', v_product.name,
            'quantity', v_quantity,
            'unit_price', v_product.price,
            'notes', v_item_notes
        );

    -- 9. RESPOSTA ESTRUTURADA EM JSON
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    RETURN jsonb_build_object(

-- ============================================================================
-- GULAS RESTAURANT PLATFORM â€” RLS DE ATUALIZAÃ‡ÃƒO DE RESTAURANTE E TAMANHO NOS ITENS
-- Migration: 20261106_rls_and_order_item_size.sql
-- Description:
--   1. Adiciona polÃ­tica RLS FOR UPDATE em public.restaurants para o staff poder
--      abrir/encerrar a receÃ§Ã£o de pedidos digitais (is_open).
--   2. Atualiza create_order_atomic para gravar o tamanho/formato (unit_quantity:
--      Normal, Familiar, 20cl, 33cl) diretamente em order_items.product_name.
-- ============================================================================

BEGIN;

-- 1. PolÃ­tica RLS para permitir ao staff autenticado atualizar o estado do seu restaurante
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Staff Update Restaurant" ON public.restaurants;
CREATE POLICY "Staff Update Restaurant"
ON public.restaurants FOR UPDATE
TO authenticated
USING (
    id IN (
        SELECT p.restaurant_id FROM public.profiles p WHERE p.id = auth.uid()
    )
)
WITH CHECK (
    id IN (
        SELECT p.restaurant_id FROM public.profiles p WHERE p.id = auth.uid()
    )
);

-- 1.5. Garantir suporte a Mesas 1 a 20 na tabela public.tables (usando o ID real do restaurante na BD)
INSERT INTO public.tables (restaurant_id, number, name, is_active)
SELECT
    r.id,
    gs.num,
    'Mesa ' || gs.num,
    true
FROM public.restaurants r
CROSS JOIN generate_series(1, 20) AS gs(num)
WHERE r.slug = 'gulas'
ON CONFLICT (restaurant_id, number) DO NOTHING;

-- 1.6. Garantir que public.restaurants e public.products estÃ£o na publicaÃ§Ã£o supabase_realtime
DO $$
BEGIN
    IF NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM pg_publication_rel pr
        JOIN pg_class c ON c.oid = pr.prrelid
        JOIN pg_publication p ON p.oid = pr.prpubid
        WHERE p.pubname = 'supabase_realtime'
        AND c.relname = 'restaurants'
    ) THEN
        ALTER PUBLICATION supabase_realtime ADD TABLE public.restaurants;
    END IF;

    IF NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM pg_publication_rel pr
        JOIN pg_class c ON c.oid = pr.prrelid
        JOIN pg_publication p ON p.oid = pr.prpubid
        WHERE p.pubname = 'supabase_realtime'
        AND c.relname = 'products'
    ) THEN
        ALTER PUBLICATION supabase_realtime ADD TABLE public.products;
    END IF;
END $$;

-- 1.7. Garantir que table_id aceita NULL (para Take-Away) e que tracking_token existe
ALTER TABLE public.orders ALTER COLUMN table_id DROP NOT NULL;
ALTER TABLE public.orders ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS tracking_token VARCHAR(64);

-- 2. Atualizar create_order_atomic para incluir unit_quantity no product_name quando aplicÃ¡vel
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.create_order_atomic(
    p_restaurant_slug TEXT,
    p_table_number INTEGER,
    p_customer_notes TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
    p_items JSONB DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public, extensions
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_restaurant RECORD;
    v_table RECORD;
    v_table_id UUID := NULL;
    v_table_name TEXT := 'Take-away';
    v_order_id UUID;
    v_order_number INTEGER;
    v_created_at TIMESTAMPTZ;
    v_tracking_token VARCHAR(64);
    v_subtotal NUMERIC(10,2) := 0.00;
    v_total NUMERIC(10,2) := 0.00;
    v_item JSONB;
    v_product_id_text TEXT;
    v_product_id UUID;
    v_quantity INTEGER;
    v_item_notes TEXT;
    v_product RECORD;
    v_order_item_id UUID;
    v_final_product_name TEXT;
    v_returned_items JSONB := '[]'::jsonb;
    v_item_response JSONB;
    v_items_count INTEGER;
BEGIN
    IF p_restaurant_slug IS NULL OR trim(p_restaurant_slug) = '' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'RESTAURANT_NOT_FOUND: Slug do restaurante nÃ£o fornecido.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    SELECT id, name, slug, is_active, is_open
    INTO v_restaurant
    FROM public.restaurants
    WHERE slug = trim(p_restaurant_slug);

    IF NOT FOUND THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'RESTAURANT_NOT_FOUND: Restaurante com o slug "%" nÃ£o foi encontrado.', p_restaurant_slug USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    IF NOT v_restaurant.is_active THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'RESTAURANT_INACTIVE: O restaurante "%" encontra-se inativo no sistema.', v_restaurant.name USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    IF NOT v_restaurant.is_open THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'RESTAURANT_CLOSED: O restaurante "%" encontra-se encerrado de momento.', v_restaurant.name USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    IF p_table_number IS NULL OR p_table_number < 0 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'TABLE_INVALID: NÃºmero da mesa invÃ¡lido.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    IF p_table_number = 0 THEN
        v_table_id := NULL;
        v_table_name := 'Take-away';
    ELSE
        SELECT id, number, name, is_active
        INTO v_table
        FROM public.tables
        WHERE restaurant_id = v_restaurant.id
          AND number = p_table_number;

        IF NOT FOUND THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'TABLE_INVALID: A Mesa % nÃ£o existe no restaurante %.', p_table_number, v_restaurant.name USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END IF;

        IF NOT v_table.is_active THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'TABLE_INVALID: A Mesa % encontra-se desativada de momento.', p_table_number USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END IF;

        v_table_id := v_table.id;
        v_table_name := v_table.name;
    END IF;

    -- Anti-spam no SQL (mÃ¡ximo 5 pedidos por mesa nos Ãºltimos 60 segundos)
    IF (
        SELECT COUNT(*)
        FROM public.orders
        WHERE restaurant_id = v_restaurant.id
          AND table_number = p_table_number
          AND created_at > NOW() - INTERVAL '60 seconds'
    ) >= 5 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED: Demasiados pedidos submetidos em simultÃ¢neo. Por favor aguarde 1 minuto.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    IF p_customer_notes IS NOT NULL AND length(trim(p_customer_notes)) > 1000 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'INVALID_NOTES: As observaÃ§Ãµes gerais excedem o limite de 1000 caracteres.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    IF p_items IS NULL OR jsonb_typeof(p_items) <> 'array' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'EMPTY_CART: Formato de artigos invÃ¡lido.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    v_items_count := jsonb_array_length(p_items);
    IF v_items_count = 0 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'EMPTY_CART: O carrinho estÃ¡ vazio.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    IF v_items_count > 50 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'CART_LIMIT_EXCEEDED: Limite mÃ¡ximo de 50 artigos distintos por pedido excedido.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    FOR v_item IN SELECT * FROM jsonb_array_elements(p_items)
    LOOP
        v_product_id_text := v_item->>'product_id';
        v_quantity := (v_item->>'quantity')::INTEGER;
        v_item_notes := v_item->>'notes';

        IF v_product_id_text IS NULL THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'PRODUCT_INVALID: Identificador de artigo ausente.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END IF;

        BEGIN
            v_product_id := v_product_id_text::UUID;
        EXCEPTION WHEN invalid_text_representation THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'PRODUCT_INVALID: Formato de UUID do artigo invÃ¡lido.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END;

        IF v_item_notes IS NOT NULL AND length(trim(v_item_notes)) > 500 THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'INVALID_NOTES: As observaÃ§Ãµes do artigo excedem o limite de 500 caracteres.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END IF;

        IF v_quantity IS NULL OR v_quantity <= 0 OR v_quantity > 50 THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'INVALID_QUANTITY: A quantidade do artigo deve estar entre 1 e 50.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END IF;

        SELECT p.id, p.name, p.price, p.cost, p.is_available, p.unit_quantity, c.is_active AS category_is_active
        INTO v_product
        FROM public.products p
        JOIN public.categories c ON c.id = p.category_id
        WHERE p.id = v_product_id
          AND p.restaurant_id = v_restaurant.id
          AND c.restaurant_id = v_restaurant.id;

        IF NOT FOUND THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'PRODUCT_INVALID: Artigo nÃ£o encontrado no menu do restaurante.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END IF;

        IF NOT v_product.is_available THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'PRODUCT_UNAVAILABLE: O artigo "%" nÃ£o se encontra disponÃ­vel de momento.', v_product.name USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END IF;

        IF NOT v_product.category_is_active THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'PRODUCT_UNAVAILABLE: A categoria do artigo "%" encontra-se indisponÃ­vel de momento.', v_product.name USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END IF;

        v_subtotal := v_subtotal + (v_product.price * v_quantity);
    END LOOP;

    v_total := v_subtotal;

    -- Reinicia automaticamente as senhas no #1 a cada novo dia operacional (Europe/Lisbon)
    INSERT INTO public.restaurant_counters (restaurant_id, last_order_number, updated_at)
    VALUES (v_restaurant.id, 1, NOW())
    ON CONFLICT (restaurant_id)
    DO UPDATE SET
        last_order_number = CASE
            WHEN (public.restaurant_counters.updated_at AT TIME ZONE 'Europe/Lisbon')::date
                 < (NOW() AT TIME ZONE 'Europe/Lisbon')::date
            THEN 1
            ELSE public.restaurant_counters.last_order_number + 1
        END,
        updated_at = NOW()
    RETURNING last_order_number INTO v_order_number;

    v_tracking_token := replace(gen_random_uuid()::text, '-', '') || replace(gen_random_uuid()::text, '-', '');

    INSERT INTO public.orders (
        restaurant_id,
        table_id,
        table_number,
        order_number,
        tracking_token,
        status,
        subtotal,
        total,
        customer_notes
    ) VALUES (
        v_restaurant.id,
        v_table_id,
        p_table_number,
        v_order_number,
        v_tracking_token,
        'pending',
        v_subtotal,
        v_total,
        p_customer_notes
    ) RETURNING id, created_at, tracking_token INTO v_order_id, v_created_at, v_tracking_token;

    FOR v_item IN SELECT * FROM jsonb_array_elements(p_items)
    LOOP
        v_product_id := (v_item->>'product_id')::UUID;
        v_quantity := (v_item->>'quantity')::INTEGER;
        v_item_notes := v_item->>'notes';

        SELECT id, name, price, cost, unit_quantity
        INTO v_product
        FROM public.products
        WHERE id = v_product_id;

        IF v_product.unit_quantity IS NOT NULL AND trim(v_product.unit_quantity) IN ('Normal', 'Familiar', '20cl', '33cl') THEN
            v_final_product_name := v_product.name || ' (' || trim(v_product.unit_quantity) || ')';
        ELSE
            v_final_product_name := v_product.name;
        END IF;

        INSERT INTO public.order_items (
            order_id,
            product_id,
            product_name,
            quantity,
            unit_price,
            unit_cost,
            notes
        ) VALUES (
            v_order_id,
            v_product.id,
            v_final_product_name,
            v_quantity,
            v_product.price,
            v_product.cost,
            v_item_notes
        ) RETURNING id INTO v_order_item_id;

        v_item_response := jsonb_build_object(
            'id', v_order_item_id,
            'product_id', v_product.id,
            'product_name', v_final_product_name,
            'quantity', v_quantity,
            'unit_price', v_product.price,
            'notes', v_item_notes
        );
        v_returned_items := v_returned_items || jsonb_build_array(v_item_response);
    END LOOP;

    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
        'success', true,
        'order', jsonb_build_object(
            'id', v_order_id,
            'restaurant_id', v_restaurant.id,
            'restaurant_slug', v_restaurant.slug,
            'restaurant_name', v_restaurant.name,
            'table_id', v_table_id,
            'table_number', p_table_number,
            'table_name', v_table_name,
            'order_number', v_order_number,
            'status', 'pending',
            'subtotal', v_subtotal,
            'total', v_total,
            'customer_notes', p_customer_notes,
            'created_at', v_created_at,
            'tracking_token', v_tracking_token,
            'items', v_returned_items
        )
    );
END;
$$;

REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.create_order_atomic(TEXT, INTEGER, TEXT, JSONB) FROM PUBLIC;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.create_order_atomic(TEXT, INTEGER, TEXT, JSONB) TO anon, authenticated, service_role;

-- 3. RPC segura para cancelamento pelo cliente enquanto o pedido estÃ¡ 'pending'
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.cancel_pending_order_by_customer(
    p_order_id UUID,
    p_tracking_token TEXT
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public, extensions
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_order RECORD;
BEGIN
    IF p_order_id IS NULL OR p_tracking_token IS NULL OR trim(p_tracking_token) = '' THEN
        RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'Credenciais de pedido invÃ¡lidas.');
    END IF;

    SELECT id, status
    INTO v_order
    FROM public.orders
    WHERE id = p_order_id
      AND tracking_token = trim(p_tracking_token)
    FOR UPDATE;

    IF NOT FOUND THEN
        RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'Pedido nÃ£o encontrado.');
    END IF;

    IF v_order.status <> 'pending' THEN
        RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'A cozinha jÃ¡ aceitou este pedido e nÃ£o pode ser cancelado aqui.');
    END IF;

    UPDATE public.orders
    SET status = 'cancelled',
        updated_at = NOW()
    WHERE id = p_order_id;

    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', true);
END;
$$;

REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.cancel_pending_order_by_customer(UUID, TEXT) FROM PUBLIC;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.cancel_pending_order_by_customer(UUID, TEXT) TO anon, authenticated, service_role;

COMMIT;

-- 4. ATUALIZAR RPC TRANSACIONAL public.create_order_atomic
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.create_order_atomic(
    p_restaurant_slug TEXT,
    p_table_number INTEGER,
    p_customer_notes TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
    p_items JSONB DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public, extensions
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_restaurant RECORD;
    v_table RECORD;
    v_table_id UUID := NULL;
    v_table_name TEXT := 'Take-away';
    v_order_id UUID;
    v_order_number INTEGER;
    v_created_at TIMESTAMPTZ;
    v_tracking_token VARCHAR(64);
    v_subtotal NUMERIC(10,2) := 0.00;
    v_total NUMERIC(10,2) := 0.00;
    v_item JSONB;
    v_product_id_text TEXT;
    v_product_id UUID;
    v_quantity INTEGER;
    v_item_notes TEXT;
    v_product RECORD;
    v_order_item_id UUID;
    v_final_product_name TEXT;
    v_returned_items JSONB := '[]'::jsonb;
    v_item_response JSONB;
    v_items_count INTEGER;

    -- VariÃ¡veis para validaÃ§Ã£o e recÃ¡lculo de modificadores
    v_modifier_ids_json JSONB;
    v_modifier_uuids UUID[];
    v_modifier_uuid_elem TEXT;
    v_parsed_uuid UUID;
    v_mods_count INTEGER;
    v_mods_price_delta NUMERIC(10,2);
    v_mods_all_active BOOLEAN;
    v_item_unit_price NUMERIC(10,2);
    v_selected_modifiers_snapshot JSONB;
    v_modifiers_summary_text TEXT;
BEGIN
    -- 1. ValidaÃ§Ã£o de slug do restaurante
    IF p_restaurant_slug IS NULL OR trim(p_restaurant_slug) = '' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'RESTAURANT_NOT_FOUND: Slug do restaurante nÃ£o fornecido.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    SELECT id, name, slug, is_active, is_open
    INTO v_restaurant
    FROM public.restaurants
    WHERE slug = trim(p_restaurant_slug);

    IF NOT FOUND THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'RESTAURANT_NOT_FOUND: Restaurante com o slug "%" nÃ£o foi encontrado.', p_restaurant_slug USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    IF NOT v_restaurant.is_active THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'RESTAURANT_INACTIVE: O restaurante "%" encontra-se inativo no sistema.', v_restaurant.name USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    IF NOT v_restaurant.is_open THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'RESTAURANT_CLOSED: O restaurante "%" encontra-se encerrado de momento.', v_restaurant.name USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    -- 2. ValidaÃ§Ã£o de mesa / Take-Away
    IF p_table_number IS NULL OR p_table_number < 0 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'TABLE_INVALID: NÃºmero da mesa invÃ¡lido.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    IF p_table_number = 0 THEN
        v_table_id := NULL;
        v_table_name := 'Take-away';
    ELSE
        SELECT id, number, name, is_active
        INTO v_table
        FROM public.tables
        WHERE restaurant_id = v_restaurant.id
          AND number = p_table_number;

        IF NOT FOUND THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'TABLE_INVALID: A Mesa % nÃ£o existe no restaurante %.', p_table_number, v_restaurant.name USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END IF;

        IF NOT v_table.is_active THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'TABLE_INVALID: A Mesa % encontra-se desativada de momento.', p_table_number USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END IF;

        v_table_id := v_table.id;
        v_table_name := v_table.name;
    END IF;

    -- 3. ValidaÃ§Ã£o das observaÃ§Ãµes gerais
    IF p_customer_notes IS NOT NULL AND length(trim(p_customer_notes)) > 1000 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'INVALID_NOTES: As observaÃ§Ãµes gerais excedem o limite de 1000 caracteres.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    -- 4. ValidaÃ§Ã£o de formato da lista de artigos
    IF p_items IS NULL OR jsonb_typeof(p_items) <> 'array' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'EMPTY_CART: Formato de artigos invÃ¡lido.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    v_items_count := jsonb_array_length(p_items);
    IF v_items_count = 0 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'EMPTY_CART: O carrinho estÃ¡ vazio.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    IF v_items_count > 50 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'CART_LIMIT_EXCEEDED: Limite mÃ¡ximo de 50 artigos distintos por pedido excedido.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;

    -- 5. PRIMEIRO PASSO: VALIDAÃ‡ÃƒO TOTAL DE PRODUTOS, MODIFICADORES E CÃLCULO DE SUBTOTAIS
    FOR v_item IN SELECT * FROM jsonb_array_elements(p_items)
    LOOP
        v_product_id_text := v_item->>'product_id';
        v_quantity := (v_item->>'quantity')::INTEGER;
        v_item_notes := v_item->>'notes';

        IF v_product_id_text IS NULL THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'PRODUCT_INVALID: Identificador de artigo ausente.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END IF;

        BEGIN
            v_product_id := v_product_id_text::UUID;
        EXCEPTION WHEN invalid_text_representation THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'PRODUCT_INVALID: Formato de UUID do artigo invÃ¡lido.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END;

                IF v_quantity IS NULL OR v_quantity <= 0 OR v_quantity > 50 THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'INVALID_QUANTITY: A quantidade do artigo deve estar entre 1 e 50.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END IF;

                IF v_item_notes IS NOT NULL AND length(trim(v_item_notes)) > 500 THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'INVALID_NOTES: As observaÃ§Ãµes do artigo excedem o limite de 500 caracteres.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END IF;


        SELECT id, name, price, cost, is_available, unit_quantity
        INTO v_product
        FROM public.products
        WHERE id = v_product_id
          AND restaurant_id = v_restaurant.id;

        IF NOT FOUND THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'PRODUCT_INVALID: Artigo nÃ£o encontrado no menu do restaurante.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END IF;

        IF NOT v_product.is_available THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'PRODUCT_UNAVAILABLE: O artigo "%" nÃ£o se encontra disponÃ­vel de momento.', v_product.name USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
        END IF;

        -- Processamento de modificadores do item
        v_modifier_ids_json := v_item->'modifier_ids';
        v_modifier_uuids := ARRAY[]::UUID[];
        v_mods_price_delta := 0.00;

        IF v_modifier_ids_json IS NOT NULL AND jsonb_typeof(v_modifier_ids_json) = 'array' AND jsonb_array_length(v_modifier_ids_json) > 0 THEN
            -- Converter elementos em array de UUID
            FOR v_modifier_uuid_elem IN SELECT jsonb_array_elements_text(v_modifier_ids_json)
            LOOP
                BEGIN
                    v_parsed_uuid := v_modifier_uuid_elem::UUID;
                EXCEPTION WHEN invalid_text_representation THEN
                    RAISE EXCEPTION 'MODIFIER_INVALID: Formato de UUID de modificador invÃ¡lido.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
                END;
                v_modifier_uuids := array_append(v_modifier_uuids, v_parsed_uuid);
            END LOOP;

            -- Verificar duplicados intra-item
            IF array_length(v_modifier_uuids, 1) <> (SELECT count(DISTINCT u) FROM unnest(v_modifier_uuids) AS u) THEN
                RAISE EXCEPTION 'DUPLICATE_MODIFIER: Modificadores duplicados no mesmo artigo.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
            END IF;

            -- Validar existÃªncia, pertenÃ§a ao produto e estado ativo
            SELECT
                count(*),
                COALESCE(sum(price_delta), 0.00),
                bool_and(active)
            INTO
                v_mods_count,
                v_mods_price_delta,
                v_mods_all_active
            FROM public.product_modifiers
            WHERE id = ANY(v_modifier_uuids)
              AND product_id = v_product_id;

            IF v_mods_count <> array_length(v_modifier_uuids, 1) THEN
                RAISE EXCEPTION 'MODIFIER_INVALID: Um ou mais modificadores nÃ£o pertencem ao artigo ou nÃ£o existem.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
            END IF;

            IF NOT v_mods_all_active THEN
                RAISE EXCEPTION 'MODIFIER_UNAVAILABLE: Um ou mais modificadores encontram-se indisponÃ­veis de momento.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
            END IF;
        END IF;

        v_item_unit_price := v_product.price + v_mods_price_delta;
        v_subtotal := v_subtotal + (v_item_unit_price * v_quantity);
    END LOOP;

    v_total := v_subtotal;

    -- 6. GERAÃ‡ÃƒO ATÃ“MICA DE NÃšMERO DIÃRIO DE PEDIDO
    INSERT INTO public.restaurant_counters (restaurant_id, last_order_number, updated_at)
    VALUES (v_restaurant.id, 1, NOW())
    ON CONFLICT (restaurant_id)
    DO UPDATE SET
        last_order_number = public.restaurant_counters.last_order_number + 1,
        updated_at = NOW()
    RETURNING last_order_number INTO v_order_number;

    -- 7. GERAÃ‡ÃƒO DE TRACKING TOKEN CRIPTOGRÃFICO
    v_tracking_token := replace(gen_random_uuid()::text, '-', '') || replace(gen_random_uuid()::text, '-', '');

    -- 8. INSERÃ‡ÃƒO DO CABEÃ‡ALHO DO PEDIDO
    INSERT INTO public.orders (
        restaurant_id,
        table_id,
        table_number,
        order_number,
        tracking_token,
        status,
        subtotal,
        total,
        customer_notes
    ) VALUES (
        v_restaurant.id,
        v_table_id,
        p_table_number,
        v_order_number,
        v_tracking_token,
        'pending',
        v_subtotal,
        v_total,
        p_customer_notes
    ) RETURNING id, created_at, tracking_token INTO v_order_id, v_created_at, v_tracking_token;

    -- 9. INSERÃ‡ÃƒO DOS ITENS (ORDER_ITEMS) COM SNAPSHOTS IMUTÃVEIS
    FOR v_item IN SELECT * FROM jsonb_array_elements(p_items)
    LOOP
        v_product_id := (v_item->>'product_id')::UUID;
        v_quantity := (v_item->>'quantity')::INTEGER;
        v_item_notes := v_item->>'notes';

        SELECT id, name, price, cost, unit_quantity
        INTO v_product
        FROM public.products
        WHERE id = v_product_id;

        IF v_product.unit_quantity IS NOT NULL AND trim(v_product.unit_quantity) IN ('Normal', 'Familiar', '20cl', '33cl') THEN
            v_final_product_name := v_product.name || ' (' || trim(v_product.unit_quantity) || ')';
        ELSE
            v_final_product_name := v_product.name;
        END IF;

        -- Extrair novamente modifier_ids para inserÃ§Ã£o e snapshots
        v_modifier_ids_json := v_item->'modifier_ids';
        v_modifier_uuids := ARRAY[]::UUID[];
        v_mods_price_delta := 0.00;
        v_selected_modifiers_snapshot := '[]'::jsonb;
        v_modifiers_summary_text := NULL;

        IF v_modifier_ids_json IS NOT NULL AND jsonb_typeof(v_modifier_ids_json) = 'array' AND jsonb_array_length(v_modifier_ids_json) > 0 THEN
            FOR v_modifier_uuid_elem IN SELECT jsonb_array_elements_text(v_modifier_ids_json)
            LOOP
                v_modifier_uuids := array_append(v_modifier_uuids, v_modifier_uuid_elem::UUID);
            END LOOP;

            -- Snapshot imutÃ¡vel dos modificadores e texto formatado para o KDS
            SELECT
                COALESCE(
                    jsonb_agg(
                        jsonb_build_object(
                            'id', pm.id,
                            'label', pm.label,
                            'group', pm."group",
                            'price_delta', pm.price_delta
                        ) ORDER BY pm.sort_order, pm.created_at
                    ),
                    '[]'::jsonb
                ),
                string_agg(pm.label, ', ' ORDER BY pm.sort_order, pm.created_at),
                COALESCE(sum(pm.price_delta), 0.00)
            INTO
                v_selected_modifiers_snapshot,
                v_modifiers_summary_text,
                v_mods_price_delta
            FROM public.product_modifiers pm
            WHERE pm.id = ANY(v_modifier_uuids)
              AND pm.product_id = v_product_id;
        END IF;

        v_item_unit_price := v_product.price + v_mods_price_delta;

        INSERT INTO public.order_items (
    order_id,
    product_id,
    product_name,
    unit_price,
    quantity,
    line_total,
    notes,
    selected_modifiers,
    modifiers_summary
            ) VALUES (
                v_order_id,
                v_product.id,
                v_final_product_name,
                v_quantity,
                v_item_unit_price,
                v_product.cost,
                v_item_notes,
                v_selected_modifiers_snapshot,
                v_modifiers_summary_text
            ) RETURNING id INTO v_order_item_id;

            v_item_response := jsonb_build_object(
                'id', v_order_item_id,
                'product_id', v_product.id,
                'product_name', v_final_product_name,
                'quantity', v_quantity,
                'unit_price', v_item_unit_price,
                'notes', v_item_notes,
                'selected_modifiers', v_selected_modifiers_snapshot,
                'modifiers_summary', v_modifiers_summary_text
            );
            v_returned_items := v_returned_items || jsonb_build_array(v_item_response);
        END LOOP;

    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
        'success', true,
        'order', jsonb_build_object(
            'id', v_order_id,
            'restaurant_id', v_restaurant.id,
            'restaurant_slug', v_restaurant.slug,
            'restaurant_name', v_restaurant.name,
            'table_id', v_table_id,
            'table_number', p_table_number,
            'table_name', v_table_name,
            'order_number', v_order_number,
            'status', 'pending',
            'subtotal', v_subtotal,
            'total', v_total,
            'customer_notes', p_customer_notes,
            'created_at', v_created_at,
            'tracking_token', v_tracking_token,
            'items', v_returned_items
        )
    );
END;
$$;

-- 5. PERMISSÃ•ES DE EXECUÃ‡ÃƒO
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.create_order_atomic(TEXT, INTEGER, TEXT, JSONB) FROM PUBLIC;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.create_order_atomic(TEXT, INTEGER, TEXT, JSONB) TO anon, authenticated, service_role;

COMMENT ON FUNCTION public.create_order_atomic(TEXT, INTEGER, TEXT, JSONB) IS
'GULAS: CriaÃ§Ã£o atÃ³mica de encomendas com suporte a modificadores selecionados, validaÃ§Ã£o Zero-Trust e persistÃªncia para KDS.';

COMMIT;

-- ============================================================================
-- GULAS RESTAURANT PLATFORM â€” TABELA DE MODIFICADORES DE PRODUTO E RLS
-- Migration: 20261110_product_modifiers.sql
-- Description:
--   1. Cria a tabela public.product_modifiers para suporte a extras pagos,
--      ingredientes removÃ­veis e molhos.
--   2. Define constraints de integridade (price_delta >= 0, group permitido).
--   3. Cria Ã­ndices de performance para consultas por produto.
--   4. Ativa Row Level Security (RLS) e define polÃ­ticas de leitura pÃºblica
--      (apenas ativos) e gestÃ£o restrita ao staff/gestÃ£o do restaurante.
--   5. Adiciona trigger para atualizaÃ§Ã£o automÃ¡tica de updated_at.
-- ============================================================================

BEGIN;

-- 1. CRIAR TABELA public.product_modifiers
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.product_modifiers (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    product_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.products(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    label VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    price_delta NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
    "group" VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
    sort_order INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    CONSTRAINT check_product_modifiers_price_delta CHECK (price_delta >= 0),
    CONSTRAINT check_product_modifiers_group CHECK ("group" IN ('extra', 'remove', 'sauce'))
);

-- 2. TRIGGER PARA updated_at (seguindo o padrÃ£o handle_updated_at existente)
DO $$
BEGIN
    IF NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_trigger
        WHERE tgname = 'set_product_modifiers_updated_at'
    ) THEN
        CREATE TRIGGER set_product_modifiers_updated_at
            BEFORE UPDATE ON public.product_modifiers
            FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.handle_updated_at();
    END IF;
END $$;

-- 3. ÃNDICES DE PERFORMANCE
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_product_modifiers_product_id
    ON public.product_modifiers(product_id)
    WHERE active = true;

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_product_modifiers_product_sort
    ON public.product_modifiers(product_id, sort_order);

-- 4. ATIVAR ROW LEVEL SECURITY (RLS)
ALTER TABLE public.product_modifiers ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- 5. POLÃTICAS RLS

-- Leitura pÃºblica: clientes consultam apenas modificadores ativos de produtos e restaurantes disponÃ­veis
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Public Read Active Product Modifiers" ON public.product_modifiers;
CREATE POLICY "Public Read Active Product Modifiers"
ON public.product_modifiers FOR SELECT
TO anon, authenticated
USING (
    active = true
    AND EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM public.products p
        JOIN public.restaurants r ON r.id = p.restaurant_id
        WHERE p.id = product_modifiers.product_id
          AND p.is_available = true
          AND r.is_active = true
    )
);

-- GestÃ£o por Staff autenticado: apenas membros de staff/gestÃ£o do restaurante dono do produto
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Staff Manage Product Modifiers" ON public.product_modifiers;
CREATE POLICY "Staff Manage Product Modifiers"
ON public.product_modifiers FOR ALL
TO authenticated
USING (
    product_id IN (
        SELECT pr.id FROM public.products pr
        JOIN public.profiles prof ON prof.restaurant_id = pr.restaurant_id
        WHERE prof.id = auth.uid()
          AND prof.role IN ('owner', 'manager', 'staff')
    )
)
WITH CHECK (
    product_id IN (
        SELECT pr.id FROM public.products pr
        JOIN public.profiles prof ON prof.restaurant_id = pr.restaurant_id
        WHERE prof.id = auth.uid()
          AND prof.role IN ('owner', 'manager', 'staff')
    )
);

-- 6. PERMISSÃ•ES DE TABELA (sem conceder TRUNCATE a authenticated)
GRANT SELECT ON public.product_modifiers TO anon;
GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON public.product_modifiers TO authenticated;
GRANT ALL ON public.product_modifiers TO service_role;

COMMIT;

-- Corrige a associaÃ§Ã£o dos modificadores aos burgers reais
-- SÃ£o Miguel Burger: 0084 -> 0019
UPDATE public.product_modifiers
SET
    product_id = 'b1111111-0019-4000-8000-000000000019'::uuid,
    id = (
        'd1111111-0019-4000-8000-0000000000'
        || lpad(sort_order::text, 2, '0')
    )::uuid
WHERE product_id = 'b1111111-0084-4000-8000-000000000084'::uuid;

-- Cheeseburger Bacon Edition: 0085 -> 0020
UPDATE public.product_modifiers
SET
    product_id = 'b1111111-0020-4000-8000-000000000020'::uuid,
    id = (
        'd1111111-0020-4000-8000-0000000000'
        || lpad(sort_order::text, 2, '0')
    )::uuid
WHERE product_id = 'b1111111-0085-4000-8000-000000000085'::uuid;

-- Gulas Burger: 0086 -> 0021
UPDATE public.product_modifiers
SET
    product_id = 'b1111111-0021-4000-8000-000000000021'::uuid,
    id = (
        'd1111111-0021-4000-8000-0000000000'
        || lpad(sort_order::text, 2, '0')
    )::uuid
WHERE product_id = 'b1111111-0086-4000-8000-000000000086'::uuid;

========== C:\Users\hugom\.antigravity-ide\gulas\supabase\migrations\20261125_rbac_kds_staff_security.sql ==========

-- ============================================================================
-- GULAS RESTAURANT PLATFORM â€” RBAC & SEGURANÃ‡A KDS (DONO vs. FUNCIONÃRIOS)
-- Migration: 20261125_rbac_kds_staff_security.sql
-- Estado: PROPOSTA LOCAL PARA APROVAÃ‡ÃƒO (NÃƒO APLICAR DIRETAMENTE NA BD REMOTA)
-- DescriÃ§Ã£o:
--   1. SeparaÃ§Ã£o explÃ­cita de operaÃ§Ãµes (SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE) em public.orders.
--   2. Staff (cozinha/sala) restrito estritamente a pedidos ativos ('pending', 'accepted', 'preparing', 'ready').
--   3. DELETE de pedidos reservado exclusivamente ao dono ('owner').
--   4. Trigger de validaÃ§Ã£o operacional: Staff sÃ³ pode executar transiÃ§Ãµes aprovadas
--      (pending -> accepted -> preparing -> ready -> completed), sendo estritamente
--      proibido de cancelar pedidos ou alterar campos administrativos e financeiros.
--   5. RestriÃ§Ã£o de catÃ¡logo, mesas e estado do restaurante exclusivamente a 'owner'.
-- ============================================================================

BEGIN;

-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 1. TRIGGER DE PROTEÃ‡ÃƒO OPERACIONAL E IMUTABILIDADE PARA STAFF EM ORDERS
-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.enforce_order_staff_update_rules()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_user_role TEXT;
BEGIN
    -- Obter o papel do utilizador autenticado
    SELECT p.role INTO v_user_role
    FROM public.profiles p
    WHERE p.id = auth.uid();

    -- Dono ('owner'), chamadas de sistema ('service_role') e triggers internos mantÃªm permissÃ£o plena
    IF v_user_role = 'owner' OR auth.role() = 'service_role' OR auth.uid() IS NULL THEN
        RETURN NEW;
    END IF;

    -- Para contas 'staff' (ou qualquer papel sem privilÃ©gios de owner):

    -- 1. Imutabilidade absoluta de campos financeiros, estruturais e de destino
    IF OLD.restaurant_id IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.restaurant_id OR
       OLD.order_number IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.order_number OR
       OLD.subtotal IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.subtotal OR
       OLD.total IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.total OR
       OLD.table_id IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.table_id OR
       OLD.customer_notes IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.customer_notes OR
       OLD.tracking_token IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.tracking_token OR
       OLD.created_at IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.created_at THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'VIOLATION_RBAC: FuncionÃ¡rios nÃ£o tÃªm permissÃ£o para alterar campos administrativos, financeiros ou estruturais do pedido.'
            USING ERRCODE = '42501';
    END IF;

    -- 2. Staff NÃƒO pode cancelar pedidos (reserva exclusiva do dono)
    IF NEW.status = 'cancelled' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'VIOLATION_RBAC: O cancelamento de pedidos Ã© restrito exclusivamente ao dono do restaurante.'
            USING ERRCODE = '42501';
    END IF;

    -- 3. Staff NÃƒO pode reabrir pedidos jÃ¡ concluÃ­dos
    IF OLD.status = 'completed' AND NEW.status != 'completed' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'VIOLATION_RBAC: Apenas o dono pode reabrir pedidos que jÃ¡ se encontram concluÃ­dos.'
            USING ERRCODE = '42501';
    END IF;

    -- 4. TransiÃ§Ãµes operacionais permitidas Ã  cozinha/sala:
    --    pending -> accepted
    --    accepted -> preparing
    --    preparing -> ready
    --    ready -> completed
    IF NOT (
        (OLD.status = 'pending' AND NEW.status IN ('accepted', 'preparing')) OR
        (OLD.status = 'accepted' AND NEW.status = 'preparing') OR
        (OLD.status = 'preparing' AND NEW.status = 'ready') OR
        (OLD.status = 'ready' AND NEW.status = 'completed') OR
        (OLD.status = NEW.status)
    ) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'VIOLATION_RBAC: TransiÃ§Ã£o operacional de estado nÃ£o autorizada para o papel de funcionÃ¡rio.'
            USING ERRCODE = '42501';
    END IF;

    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trigger_enforce_order_staff_update_rules ON public.orders;
CREATE TRIGGER trigger_enforce_order_staff_update_rules
    BEFORE UPDATE ON public.orders
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION public.enforce_order_staff_update_rules();


-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 2. REFORÃ‡O DE RLS NA TABELA PUBLIC.ORDERS
-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Staff Manage Orders" ON public.orders;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Owner Read Orders" ON public.orders;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Staff Read Active Orders" ON public.orders;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Owner Insert Orders" ON public.orders;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Owner Update Orders" ON public.orders;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Staff Update Active Orders" ON public.orders;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Owner Delete Orders" ON public.orders;

-- SELECT: Dono consulta todos os pedidos (ativos e histÃ³ricos) do seu restaurante
CREATE POLICY "Owner Read Orders"
ON public.orders FOR SELECT
TO authenticated
USING (
    restaurant_id IN (
        SELECT p.restaurant_id FROM public.profiles p
        WHERE p.id = auth.uid() AND p.role = 'owner'
    )
);

-- SELECT: Staff consulta apenas pedidos ativos em preparaÃ§Ã£o na bancada
CREATE POLICY "Staff Read Active Orders"
ON public.orders FOR SELECT
TO authenticated
USING (
    status IN ('pending', 'accepted', 'preparing', 'ready')
    AND restaurant_id IN (
        SELECT p.restaurant_id FROM public.profiles p
        WHERE p.id = auth.uid() AND p.role = 'staff'
    )
);

-- INSERT: InserÃ§Ã£o direta permitida apenas a Owner (pedidos pÃºblicos entram via RPC create_order_atomic)
CREATE POLICY "Owner Insert Orders"
ON public.orders FOR INSERT
TO authenticated
WITH CHECK (
    restaurant_id IN (
        SELECT p.restaurant_id FROM public.profiles p
        WHERE p.id = auth.uid() AND p.role = 'owner'
    )
);

-- UPDATE: Dono pode atualizar qualquer pedido do seu restaurante
CREATE POLICY "Owner Update Orders"
ON public.orders FOR UPDATE
TO authenticated
USING (
    restaurant_id IN (
        SELECT p.restaurant_id FROM public.profiles p
        WHERE p.id = auth.uid() AND p.role = 'owner'
    )
)
WITH CHECK (
    restaurant_id IN (
        SELECT p.restaurant_id FROM public.profiles p
        WHERE p.id = auth.uid() AND p.role = 'owner'
    )
);

-- UPDATE: Staff atualiza apenas pedidos ativos (o trigger garante que sÃ³ altera status operacional)
CREATE POLICY "Staff Update Active Orders"
ON public.orders FOR UPDATE
TO authenticated
USING (
    status IN ('pending', 'accepted', 'preparing', 'ready')
    AND restaurant_id IN (
        SELECT p.restaurant_id FROM public.profiles p
        WHERE p.id = auth.uid() AND p.role = 'staff'
    )
)
WITH CHECK (
    restaurant_id IN (
        SELECT p.restaurant_id FROM public.profiles p
        WHERE p.id = auth.uid() AND p.role = 'staff'
    )
);

-- DELETE: Reservado exclusivamente ao Dono
CREATE POLICY "Owner Delete Orders"
ON public.orders FOR DELETE
TO authenticated
USING (
    restaurant_id IN (
        SELECT p.restaurant_id FROM public.profiles p
        WHERE p.id = auth.uid() AND p.role = 'owner'
    )
);


-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 3. REFORÃ‡O DE RLS NA TABELA PUBLIC.ORDER_ITEMS
-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Staff Manage Order Items" ON public.order_items;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Owner Read Order Items" ON public.order_items;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Staff Read Active Order Items" ON public.order_items;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Owner Manage Order Items" ON public.order_items;

-- SELECT: Dono consulta todos os itens de pedidos do seu restaurante
CREATE POLICY "Owner Read Order Items"
ON public.order_items FOR SELECT
TO authenticated
USING (
    order_id IN (
        SELECT o.id FROM public.orders o
        JOIN public.profiles p ON p.restaurant_id = o.restaurant_id
        WHERE p.id = auth.uid() AND p.role = 'owner'
    )
);

-- SELECT: Staff consulta itens apenas dos pedidos ativos na cozinha
CREATE POLICY "Staff Read Active Order Items"
ON public.order_items FOR SELECT
TO authenticated
USING (
    order_id IN (
        SELECT o.id FROM public.orders o
        JOIN public.profiles p ON p.restaurant_id = o.restaurant_id
        WHERE p.id = auth.uid() AND p.role = 'staff'
        AND o.status IN ('pending', 'accepted', 'preparing', 'ready')
    )
);

-- GESTÃƒO (INSERT / UPDATE / DELETE): Exclusiva ao Dono
CREATE POLICY "Owner Manage Order Items"
ON public.order_items FOR ALL
TO authenticated
USING (
    order_id IN (
        SELECT o.id FROM public.orders o
        JOIN public.profiles p ON p.restaurant_id = o.restaurant_id
        WHERE p.id = auth.uid() AND p.role = 'owner'
    )
)
WITH CHECK (
    order_id IN (
        SELECT o.id FROM public.orders o
        JOIN public.profiles p ON p.restaurant_id = o.restaurant_id
        WHERE p.id = auth.uid() AND p.role = 'owner'
    )
);


-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 4. REFORÃ‡O DE GESTÃƒO DO CATÃLOGO E RESTAURANTE (PRODUTOS, MESAS, CATEGORIAS)
-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- AtualizaÃ§Ã£o do restaurante (abrir/fechar receÃ§Ã£o) restrita ao Dono
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Staff Update Restaurant" ON public.restaurants;
CREATE POLICY "Owner Update Restaurant"
ON public.restaurants FOR UPDATE
TO authenticated
USING (
    id IN (
        SELECT p.restaurant_id FROM public.profiles p
        WHERE p.id = auth.uid() AND p.role = 'owner'
    )
)
WITH CHECK (
    id IN (
        SELECT p.restaurant_id FROM public.profiles p
        WHERE p.id = auth.uid() AND p.role = 'owner'
    )
);

-- GestÃ£o de produtos restrita ao Dono
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Staff Manage Products" ON public.products;
CREATE POLICY "Owner Manage Products"
ON public.products FOR ALL
TO authenticated
USING (
    restaurant_id IN (
        SELECT p.restaurant_id FROM public.profiles p
        WHERE p.id = auth.uid() AND p.role = 'owner'
    )
)
WITH CHECK (
    restaurant_id IN (
        SELECT p.restaurant_id FROM public.profiles p
        WHERE p.id = auth.uid() AND p.role = 'owner'
    )
);

COMMIT;
